                {"label":"نوع الوقود","key":"fuel_type_id","type":"number","required":true,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"السعة باللتر","key":"capacity_liters","type":"number","required":true,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الحد الأدنى","key":"minimum_level","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الحد الأعلى","key":"maximum_level","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الكمية الحالية","key":"current_quantity","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"السعة القابلة للاستخدام","key":"usable_capacity","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الحجم الميت","key":"dead_volume","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"شكل الخزان","key":"tank_shape","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الطول (م)","key":"length_meters","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"القطر (م)","key":"diameter_meters","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الارتفاع (م)","key":"height_meters","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الموقع","key":"location","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"تاريخ التركيب","key":"installation_date","type":"date","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الشركة المصنعة","key":"manufacturer","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الرقم التسلسلي","key":"serial_number","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الموديل","key":"model","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"السيريال التسلسلي للحساس","key":"sensor_serial","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"نوع الحساس","key":"sensor_type","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"تاريخ معايرة الحساس","key":"sensor_calibration_date","type":"date","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"دقة الحساس","key":"sensor_accuracy","type":"number","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"كاشف التسرب","key":"leak_detection","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"حماية تجاوز السعة","key":"overfill_protection","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"صمام الطوارئ","key":"emergency_valve","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"تاريخ آخر فحص","key":"last_inspection_date","type":"date","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"تاريخ الفحص القادم","key":"next_inspection_date","type":"date","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"شهادة الفحص","key":"inspection_certificate","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"الحالة","key":"status","type":"select","required":false,"placeholder":""},
                {"label":"سبب الحالة","key":"status_reason","type":"text","required":false,"placeholder":""}
            ],
            "valueFields":["capacity_liters","minimum_level","maximum_level","current_quantity"],
            "titleFields":["tank_code","tank_name","tank_name_ar"],
            "subtitleFields":["tank_code","tank_name","tank_name_ar","station_id","fuel_type_id","capacity_liters","minimum_level","maximum_level"],
            "createMethod":"saveTankRecord",
            "updateMethod":"updateTankRecord",
            "deleteMethod":"deleteTankRecord",
            "note":"",
            "mode":"list",
            "objectMode":false,
            "updateAction":"tankQuantity",
            "reportKind":"operational:Tank",
            "aiMethod":null,
            "reports":["الكل","نشط","صيانة","فارغ","معطل"],
            "reportStatusMap":["", "active", "maintenance", "empty", "retired"],
            "resolveMethod":null,
            "operationKey":"tanks"
        };
        const bridge = window.AndroidInterface;
        const state = { rows: [], filtered: [], editing: null, filter: '0', loadedAt: null };
        const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
        const esc = (value) => String(value == null ? '—' : value).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
        const today = () => businessDayDate();

        function toast(message, bad=false) {
            const node=$('toast');
            node.textContent=message;
            node.className='toast show';
            node.style.background=bad?'var(--danger)':'var(--toast-bg)';
            node.style.color=bad?'#fff':'var(--text-primary)';
            clearTimeout(node._timer);
            node._timer=setTimeout(()=>node.classList.remove('show'),3500);
        }
        function setLoading(value) { $('loadingIndicator').classList.toggle('show', Boolean(value)); }
        function parse(raw) {
            if (raw == null) throw new Error('لم يرجع الجسر نتيجة');
            const value=typeof raw==='string'?JSON.parse(raw):raw;
            if (value && value.success === false) throw new Error(value.error || value.message || 'فشلت العملية');
            return value;
        }
        function dataOf(value) { return value && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value,'data') ? value.data : value; }
        function invoke(method,args=[]) {
            if (!bridge || typeof bridge[method] !== 'function') return Promise.reject(new Error('العقد '+method+' غير متاح في Android Bridge'));
            try { return Promise.resolve(parse(bridge[method](...args))); }
            catch (error) { return Promise.reject(error); }
        }
        function argsForLoad() {
            if (CFG.operationKey) return [JSON.stringify({limit: 500, search: $('searchInput').value || ''})];
            if (CFG.reportKind === 'balanceSheet') return [today(), 0];
            if (CFG.reportKind === 'eod') return [];
            return CFG.listArgs || [];
        }
        function rowsFrom(value) {
            const raw=dataOf(value);
            if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw;
            if (raw && Array.isArray(raw.rows)) return raw.rows;
            if (raw && Array.isArray(raw.data)) return raw.data;
            if (CFG.objectMode && raw && typeof raw === 'object') return Object.entries(raw).map(([key,val])=>({key,value:val}));
            if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && raw.id !== undefined) return [raw];
            return [];
        }
        function field(row, names) {
            for (const name of (names||[])) {
                if (row && row[name] !== undefined && row[name] !== null && row[name] !== '') return row[name];
            }
            return '';
        }
        function titleOf(row) {
            return field(row, CFG.titleFields) || field(row,['name','title','label','id','key']) || 'سجل بلا اسم';
        }
        function subtitleOf(row) {
            return (CFG.subtitleFields||[])
                .map(k => { const v=row&&row[k]; return v===undefined||v===null||v===''?null:k+': '+v; })
                .filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
        }
        function idOf(row) {
            return field(row,['id','_id','tank_id','pump_id','vehicle_id','payment_id','shift_id','sale_id','request_id','asset_id','employee_id']);
        }
        function numeric(v) { const n=Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n)?n:0; }
        function setDateDefaults() {
            $('reportFrom').value = $('reportFrom').value || today();
            $('reportTo').value = $('reportTo').value || today();
        }

        // ============================================================
        // الفلاتر والتبويبات - مرتبطة بحالات الخزان الفعلية
        // ============================================================
        function renderFilters() {
            const labels = CFG.reports || ['الكل'];
            const icons = [
                'fa-list',
                'fa-check-circle',
                'fa-wrench',
                'fa-battery-empty',
                'fa-ban'
            ];
            const chips = $('filterChips');
            chips.innerHTML = labels.map((label,i)=>`<button type="button" class="filter-chip ${i===0?'active':''}" data-filter="${i}"><i class="fas ${icons[i]||'fa-circle'}"></i> ${esc(label)}</button>`).join('');
            chips.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
                chips.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));
                btn.classList.add('active');
                state.filter = btn.dataset.filter;
                applyFilter();
            }));
            const nav = $('bottomNav');
            nav.innerHTML = labels.map((label,i)=>`<button type="button" class="nav-item ${i===0?'active':''}" data-bottom-filter="${i}"><i class="fas ${icons[i]||'fa-circle'}"></i><span>${esc(label)}</span></button>`).join('');
            nav.querySelectorAll('[data-bottom-filter]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
                nav.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));
                btn.classList.add('active');
                state.filter = btn.dataset.bottomFilter;
                // مزامنة التصفية العلوية مع السفلية
                chips.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('active'));
                const top = chips.querySelector(`[data-filter="${state.filter}"]`);
                if (top) top.classList.add('active');
                applyFilter();
            }));
        }

        function applyFilter() {
            const q = ($('searchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
            const statusMap = CFG.reportStatusMap || [];
            const idx = parseInt(state.filter, 10);
            const statusKey = statusMap[isNaN(idx) ? 0 : idx] || '';
            state.filtered = state.rows.filter(row => {
                if (q) {
                    const text = JSON.stringify(row || {}).toLowerCase();
                    if (!text.includes(q)) return false;
                }
                if (statusKey) {
                    const rowStatus = String(field(row, ['status','state']) || '').toLowerCase();
                    if (rowStatus !== statusKey) return false;
                }
                return true;
            });
            renderCards();
        }

        function renderStats() {
            const rows = state.filtered;
            $('statCount').textContent = rows.length.toLocaleString('ar');
            $('itemCount').textContent = rows.length.toLocaleString('ar');
            const active = rows.filter(row => {
                const s = String(field(row,['status','state']) || '').toLowerCase();
                return s === 'active';
            }).length;
            $('statActive').textContent = active.toLocaleString('ar');
            let total = 0;
            (CFG.valueFields||[]).forEach(k => total += rows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+numeric(row&&row[k]),0));
            $('statTotal').textContent = total ? total.toLocaleString('ar',{maximumFractionDigits:2}) : '—';
            $('statUpdated').textContent = state.loadedAt ? state.loadedAt.toLocaleTimeString('ar',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}) : '—';
        }

        function statusBadge(status) {
            const s = String(status||'').toLowerCase();
            const map = {
                'active': { label: 'نشط', cls: 'active' },
                'maintenance': { label: 'صيانة', cls: 'inactive' },
                'empty': { label: 'فارغ', cls: 'inactive' },
                'retired': { label: 'معطل', cls: 'inactive' }
            };
            const info = map[s] || { label: status || '—', cls: 'inactive' };
            return `<span class="card-status ${info.cls}">${esc(info.label)}</span>`;
        }

        function renderCards() {
            renderStats();
            const box = $('cardsContainer');
            if (!state.filtered.length) {
                box.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-inbox"></i><p>${CFG.mode==='unsupported'?'لا توجد عملية مدعومة لهذا المسار في العقد الحالي.':'لا توجد نتائج مطابقة أو لم تُرجع قاعدة البيانات سجلات.'}</p></div>`;
                return;
            }
            box.innerHTML = state.filtered.map((row,index)=>{
                const id = esc(idOf(row)||index);
                const fields = (CFG.subtitleFields||[]).filter(k=>row && row[k]!==undefined && row[k]!==null && row[k]!=='').slice(0,6);
                const status = field(row,['status','state']);
                const buttons = `
                    <div class="action-row">
                        <button class="btn" type="button" data-view="${id}"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i> عرض</button>
                        ${CFG.updateMethod?`<button class="btn warning" type="button" data-edit="${id}"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل</button>`:''}
                        ${CFG.deleteMethod?`<button class="btn danger" type="button" data-delete="${id}"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i> حذف</button>`:''}
                    </div>`;
                return `<article class="data-card">
                    <div class="data-card-head">
                        <div style="flex:1">
                            <div class="data-card-title">${esc(titleOf(row))}</div>
                            <div class="data-card-subtitle">${esc(subtitleOf(row))}</div>
                        </div>
                        ${status?statusBadge(status):''}
                    </div>
                    <div class="data-fields">${fields.map(k=>`<div class="data-field"><span>${esc(k)}</span><strong>${esc(row[k])}</strong></div>`).join('')}</div>
                    ${buttons}
                </article>`;
            }).join('');
            box.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>openDetails(b.dataset.view)));
            box.querySelectorAll('[data-edit]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>openForm(findRow(b.dataset.edit))));
            box.querySelectorAll('[data-delete]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>deleteRow(findRow(b.dataset.delete))));
        }

        function findRow(id) { return state.rows.find((row,index)=>String(idOf(row)||index)===String(id)); }

        function openDetails(id) {
            const row = findRow(id);
            if (!row) return;
            $('detailsBody').innerHTML = `
                <div class="detail-grid">
                    ${Object.entries(row).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="detail-item"><span>${esc(k)}</span><strong>${esc(typeof v==='object'?JSON.stringify(v):v)}</strong></div>`).join('')}
                </div>
                <details style="margin-top:14px"><summary>JSON الخام</summary><pre class="raw-json">${esc(JSON.stringify(row,null,2))}</pre></details>`;
            openModal(null,'detailsModal');
        }

        // ============================================================
        // بناء النموذج - عمودان ما عدا الحقول الطويلة
        // ============================================================
        const FULL_WIDTH_KEYS = ['tank_name', 'remarks', 'extra_data', 'notes'];
        const HIDDEN_KEYS = ['tank_code', 'tank_name_ar'];

        function inputHtml(def, row) {
            const value = row && row[def.key] !== undefined ? row[def.key] : '';
            const req = def.required ? 'required' : '';
            const placeholder = def.placeholder ? `placeholder="${esc(def.placeholder)}"` : '';
            const isFull = def.type === 'textarea' || FULL_WIDTH_KEYS.includes(def.key);
            const isHidden = HIDDEN_KEYS.includes(def.key);
            const groupClass = `form-group${isFull?' full':''}${isHidden?' hidden-field':''}`;

            if (def.type === 'textarea') {
                return `<div class="${groupClass}"><label class="form-label">${esc(def.label)}${def.required?' *':''}</label><textarea class="form-input" data-key="${esc(def.key)}" rows="3" ${req} ${placeholder}>${esc(value)}</textarea></div>`;
            }

            if (def.type === 'select') {
                // الحالة: خياري نشط/غير نشط فقط
                if (def.key === 'status') {
                    const v = String(value||'').toLowerCase();
                    const isActive = v === 'active';
                    return `<div class="${groupClass}"><label class="form-label">${esc(def.label)}${def.required?' *':''}</label>
                        <select class="form-input" data-key="${esc(def.key)}" ${req}>
                            <option value="">اختر...</option>
                            <option value="active" ${isActive?'selected':''}>نشط</option>
                            <option value="inactive" ${!isActive&&v?'selected':''}>غير نشط</option>
                        </select></div>`;
                }
                // أي نوع select آخر: نترك القيم الافتراضية
                return `<div class="${groupClass}"><label class="form-label">${esc(def.label)}${def.required?' *':''}</label>
                    <select class="form-input" data-key="${esc(def.key)}" ${req}>
                        <option value="">اختر...</option>
                        <option value="active">نشط</option>
                        <option value="inactive">غير نشط</option>
                        <option value="maintenance">صيانة</option>
                        <option value="empty">فارغ</option>
                        <option value="retired">معطل</option>
                    </select></div>`;
            }

            // المحطة ونوع الوقود: يتم استبدالهما بـ select عند فتح النموذج
            if (def.key === 'station_id' || def.key === 'fuel_type_id') {
                return `<div class="${groupClass}"><label class="form-label">${esc(def.label)}${def.required?' *':''}</label>
                    <select class="form-input" data-key="${esc(def.key)}" data-needs-options="true" ${req}>
                        <option value="">جاري التحميل...</option>
                    </select></div>`;
            }

            return `<div class="${groupClass}"><label class="form-label">${esc(def.label)}${def.required?' *':''}</label>
                <input class="form-input" data-key="${esc(def.key)}" type="${esc(def.type)}" value="${esc(value)}" ${req} ${placeholder}></div>`;
        }

        async function populateFormSelects(row) {
            // المحطات
            try {
                const stations = await fetchStations();
                const el = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="station_id"]');
                if (el) {
                    el.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر المحطة...</option>' +
                        stations.map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.id)}">${esc(s.station_name_ar || s.station_name || s.station_code || ('محطة '+s.id))}</option>`).join('');
                    const v = row && row.station_id ? String(row.station_id) : (stations[0] ? String(stations[0].id) : '');
                    if (v) el.value = v;
                }
            } catch (e) { console.warn('stations load failed', e); }

            // أنواع الوقود
            try {
                const fuels = await fetchFuelTypes();
                const el = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="fuel_type_id"]');
                if (el) {
                    el.innerHTML = '<option value="">اختر نوع الوقود...</option>' +
                        fuels.map(f=>`<option value="${esc(f.id||f.fuel_type_id)}">${esc(f.fuel_name_ar || f.fuel_name || f.fuel_code || ('وقود '+(f.id||f.fuel_type_id)))}</option>`).join('');
                    const v = row && row.fuel_type_id ? String(row.fuel_type_id) : (fuels[0] ? String(fuels[0].id||fuels[0].fuel_type_id) : '');
                    if (v) el.value = v;
                }
            } catch (e) { console.warn('fuelTypes load failed', e); }

            // الحالة: مطابقة القيم القديمة (maintenance/empty/retired) مع (غير نشط)
            const statusEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="status"]');
            if (statusEl && row && row.status) {
                const s = String(row.status).toLowerCase();
                statusEl.value = s === 'active' ? 'active' : 'inactive';
            }
        }

        // توليد كود تلقائي
        function generateTankCode() {
            const d = new Date();
            const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
            const stamp = `${d.getFullYear()}${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}${pad(d.getDate())}${pad(d.getHours())}${pad(d.getMinutes())}${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
            return `TANK-${stamp}`;
        }

        function bindFormFieldBehaviors() {
            // نقل التركيز للحقل الحالي
            document.querySelectorAll('#recordForm .form-input').forEach(el => {
                el.addEventListener('focus', () => {
                    // تأخير بسيط للتأكد من انتقال المؤشر
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        try { el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length, el.value.length); } catch(_) {}
                    }, 50);
                });
            });

            // نسخ tank_name إلى tank_name_ar
            const nameEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="tank_name"]');
            const nameArEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="tank_name_ar"]');
            if (nameEl && nameArEl) {
                nameEl.addEventListener('input', () => { nameArEl.value = nameEl.value; });
                nameArEl.addEventListener('input', () => { /* لا يُعدّل المستخدم الحقل المخفي */ });
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // جلب البيانات المرجعية من قاعدة البيانات
        // ============================================================
        async function fetchStations() {
            try {
                const result = await invoke('getStations', []);
                return rowsFrom(result);
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('fetchStations failed', e);
                return [];
            }
        }
        async function fetchFuelTypes() {
            try {
                const result = await invoke('getFuelTypes', []);
                return rowsFrom(result);
            } catch (e) {
                console.warn('fetchFuelTypes failed', e);
                return [];
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // فتح/إغلاق النموذج
        // ============================================================
        async function openForm(row=null) {
            if (!CFG.createMethod && !CFG.updateMethod) {
                toast('لا توجد عملية كتابة مدعومة لهذه الشاشة', true);
                return;
            }
            state.editing = row;
            $('formTitle').textContent = row ? 'تعديل الخزان' : 'إضافة خزان جديد';
            $('recordForm').innerHTML = (CFG.fields || []).map(def => inputHtml(def, row)).join('');

            // إعداد الخزانات المخفية
            const codeEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="tank_code"]');
            if (codeEl) {
                if (!row) codeEl.value = generateTankCode();
            }
            const nameEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="tank_name"]');
            const nameArEl = document.querySelector('#recordForm [data-key="tank_name_ar"]');
            if (row && nameArEl && !nameArEl.value) nameArEl.value = row.tank_name || '';

            // تعبئة القوائم المنسدلة
            await populateFormSelects(row);

            // ربط السلوكيات
            bindFormFieldBehaviors();

            // التركيز على أول حقل مرئي
            const firstVisible = document.querySelector('#recordForm .form-group:not(.hidden-field) .form-input');
            if (firstVisible) setTimeout(()=>firstVisible.focus(), 150);

            $('formModal').classList.add('show');
        }

        function collectForm() {
            const payload = {};
            document.querySelectorAll('#recordForm [data-key]').forEach(el => {
                let value = el.value;
                const type = el.type;
                if (type === 'number') value = value === '' ? 0 : Number(value);
                payload[el.dataset.key] = value;
            });
            // نسخ إلزامي من tank_name إلى tank_name_ar
            if (payload.tank_name) payload.tank_name_ar = payload.tank_name;
            // الحالة: مطابقة للقيم المخزنة في SQLite
            if (payload.status === 'inactive') payload.status = 'maintenance';
            return payload;
        }

        async function saveForm() {
            const form = $('recordForm');
            if (!form.reportValidity()) return;
            const payload = collectForm();
            setLoading(true);
            try {
                let result;
                if (state.editing && CFG.updateMethod) {
                    const id = idOf(state.editing);
                    if (!id) throw new Error('معرف السجل غير صالح');
                    if (CFG.updateAction === 'tankQuantity') {
                        // تحديث الكمية الحالية فقط + باقي الحقول
                        result = await invoke(CFG.updateMethod, [Number(id), JSON.stringify(payload)]);
                    } else {
                        result = await invoke(CFG.updateMethod, [Number(id), JSON.stringify(payload)]);
                    }
                } else if (CFG.createMethod) {
                    result = await invoke(CFG.createMethod, [JSON.stringify(payload)]);
                } else {
                    throw new Error('لا توجد عملية حفظ مدعومة');
                }
                parse(result);
                toast('تم تنفيذ العملية والتحقق من نجاحها');
                closeModal(null, 'formModal');
                await reloadData();
            } catch (error) {
                toast(error.message || 'فشلت العملية', true);
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        async function deleteRow(row) {
            const id = row && idOf(row);
            if (!id || !CFG.deleteMethod) return;
            if (!confirm('هل تريد تنفيذ الحذف الفعلي لهذا السجل؟')) return;
            setLoading(true);
            try {
                const result = await invoke(CFG.deleteMethod, [Number(id)]);
                parse(result);
                toast('تم الحذف فعلياً');
                await reloadData();
            } catch (error) {
                toast(error.message || 'فشل الحذف', true);
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // تحميل البيانات
        // ============================================================
        async function reloadData() {
            if (CFG.mode === 'unsupported' || CFG.mode === 'ai') { renderCards(); return; }
            if (!CFG.listMethod) { renderCards(); return; }
            if (!bridge) { $('offlineBanner').classList.add('show'); renderCards(); return; }
            setLoading(true);
            try {
                const result = await invoke(CFG.listMethod, argsForLoad());
                state.rows = rowsFrom(result);
                state.loadedAt = new Date();
                $('offlineBanner').classList.remove('show');
                applyFilter();
            } catch (error) {
                state.rows = [];
                state.loadedAt = new Date();
                renderCards();
                toast(error.message || 'فشل تحميل البيانات', true);
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        function openModal(event, id) {
            if (event && event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
            $(id).classList.add('show');
        }
        function closeModal(event, id) {
            if (event && event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
            $(id).classList.remove('show');
        }

        // ============================================================
        // التقارير
        // ============================================================
        async function populateReportFilters() {
            try {
                const stations = await fetchStations();
                const sel = $('reportStationFilter');
                if (sel) sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">كل المحطات</option>' +
                    stations.map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s.id)}">${esc(s.station_name_ar || s.station_name || s.station_code || ('محطة '+s.id))}</option>`).join('');
            } catch(_) {}
            try {
                const fuels = await fetchFuelTypes();
                const sel = $('reportFuelFilter');
                if (sel) sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">كل الأنواع</option>' +
                    fuels.map(f=>`<option value="${esc(f.id||f.fuel_type_id)}">${esc(f.fuel_name_ar || f.fuel_name || f.fuel_code)}</option>`).join('');
            } catch(_) {}
        }

        function openReport() {
            setDateDefaults();
            $('reportResults').innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-file-alt"></i><p>اضغط توليد التقرير لعرض البيانات المحملة من SQLite.</p></div>';
            populateReportFilters().then(()=>openModal(null,'reportModal'));
        }

        async function generateReport() {
            setLoading(true);
            try {
                let result;
                if (CFG.reportKind === 'balanceSheet') {
                    result = await invoke('getBalanceSheet', [($('reportTo').value||today()), 0]);
                } else if (CFG.reportKind === 'eod') {
                    result = await invoke('getEodReport', []);
                } else if (String(CFG.reportKind||'').startsWith('operational:')) {
                    const suffix = String(CFG.reportKind).split(':')[1];
                    const payload = {
                        from_date: $('reportFrom').value || '',
                        to_date: $('reportTo').value || '',
                        search: $('searchInput').value || '',
                        station_id: $('reportStationFilter') ? $('reportStationFilter').value : '',
                        fuel_type_id: $('reportFuelFilter') ? $('reportFuelFilter').value : '',
                        status: $('reportStatusFilter') ? $('reportStatusFilter').value : '',
                        report_type: $('reportKindFilter') ? $('reportKindFilter').value : 'detailed',
                        include_archived: true,
                        limit: 1000
                    };
                    result = await invoke('generate' + suffix + 'Report', [JSON.stringify(payload)]);
                } else {
                    result = { data: state.filtered };
                }
                $('reportResults').innerHTML = renderReport(rowsFrom(result));
                toast('تم توليد التقرير من المصدر الفعلي');
            } catch (e) {
                toast(e.message || 'فشل التقرير', true);
            } finally {
                setLoading(false);
            }
        }

        function renderReport(rows) {
            if (!rows.length) return '<div class="empty-state">لا توجد بيانات للتقرير.</div>';
            return `<div class="data-fields">${rows.slice(0,200).map(row=>`
                <div class="data-field">
                    <span>${esc(titleOf(row))}</span>
                    <strong>${esc(subtitleOf(row)||JSON.stringify(row))}</strong>
                </div>`).join('')}</div>`;
        }

        function exportData() {
            const rows = state.filtered;
            if (!rows.length) { toast('لا توجد بيانات حقيقية لتصديرها', true); return; }
            const keys = [...new Set(rows.flatMap(row => Object.keys(row || {})))];
            const csv = '\ufeff' + [keys, ...rows.map(row => keys.map(k => String(row[k] ?? '').replace(/"/g,'""')))]
                .map(row => row.map(v => `"${v}"`).join(',')).join('\n');
            const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            const d = new Date();
            const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
            const stamp = `${d.getFullYear()}${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}${pad(d.getDate())}_${pad(d.getHours())}${pad(d.getMinutes())}${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
            a.href = url;
            a.download = `tanks_${stamp}.csv`;
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            setTimeout(()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(url); a.remove();}, 500);
            toast('تم إنشاء ملف CSV من البيانات المحملة فعلياً');
        }

        // ============================================================
        // التنقل والوضع
        // ============================================================
        function goHome() { window.location.href = '../main.html'; }

        function applyTheme() {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light';
            if (saved === 'dark') document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'dark';
            $('themeIcon').className = saved === 'dark' ? 'fas fa-sun' : 'fas fa-moon';
        }
        function toggleTheme() {
            const dark = document.documentElement.dataset.theme === 'dark';
            if (dark) delete document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
            else document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'dark';
            localStorage.setItem('theme', dark ? 'light' : 'dark');
            $('themeIcon').className = dark ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
        }

        // ============================================================
        // القائمة الجانبية - فتح/إغلاق + إخفاء اللسان
        // ============================================================
        function openSidebar() {
            const side = $('sidebar');
            const toggle = $('sidebarToggle');
            side.classList.add('open');
            toggle.classList.add('hidden');
        }
        function closeSidebar() {
            const side = $('sidebar');
            const toggle = $('sidebarToggle');
            side.classList.remove('open');
            toggle.classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function initSidebar() {
            $('sidebarToggle').addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
                e.stopPropagation();
                if ($('sidebar').classList.contains('open')) closeSidebar();
                else openSidebar();
            });
            document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-btn').forEach(btn=>{
                btn.addEventListener('click', ()=>{
                    const action = btn.dataset.sidebarAction;
                    closeSidebar();
                    setTimeout(()=>{
                        if (action === 'reload') reloadData();
                        else if (action === 'report') openReport();
                        else if (action === 'export') exportData();
                        else if (action === 'print') window.print();
                        else if (action === 'home') goHome();
                    }, 120);
                });
            });
            // إغلاق عند النقر خارج القائمة
            document.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
                const side = $('sidebar');
                const toggle = $('sidebarToggle');
                if (!side.classList.contains('open')) return;
                if (side.contains(e.target) || toggle.contains(e.target)) return;
                closeSidebar();
            });
            document.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{
                if (e.key === 'Escape') {
                    closeSidebar();
                    document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('show'));
                }
            });
        }

        // ============================================================
        // ربط الأحداث
        // ============================================================
        function bindGlobalEvents() {
            $('searchInput').addEventListener('input', applyFilter);
            $('themeToggle').addEventListener('click', toggleTheme);
            $('fab').addEventListener('click', ()=>openForm());
            $('saveBtn').addEventListener('click', saveForm);
            window.addEventListener('online', ()=>toast('عاد اتصال الجسر المحتمل؛ اضغط تحديث للتحقق'));
            window.addEventListener('offline', ()=>$('offlineBanner').classList.add('show'));
        }

        // تصدير الدوال إلى window لاستخدامها في onclick
        Object.assign(window, {
            goHome, openReport, exportData, reloadData, openForm, saveForm,
            openModal, closeModal
        });

        // ============================================================
        // التهيئة
        // ============================================================
        applyTheme();
        renderFilters();
        initSidebar();
        bindGlobalEvents();
        reloadData();
    })();
    </script>
    <script src="file:///android_asset/assets-local/js/modal-interaction.js"></script>

    <script src="file:///android_asset/assets-local/js/export-runtime.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

            .card-info-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
        }

        /* Print styles */
        body.invoice-print-mode > * { visibility: hidden !important; }
        body.invoice-print-mode #supplyInvoice,
        body.invoice-print-mode #supplyInvoice * { visibility: visible !important; }
        body.invoice-print-mode #supplyInvoice {
            display: block !important;
            position: absolute !important;
            inset: 0 !important;
            margin: 0 !important;
            width: 210mm !important;
            min-height: 297mm !important;
            box-shadow: none !important;
        }
        @page { size: A4; margin: 0; }
    </style>

<!-- حاوية الفاتورة للطباعة -->
<section id="supplyInvoice" aria-label="فاتورة توريد وقود"></section>

<script>
(() => {
    'use strict';

    const STATUS_LABELS = {
        'pending': { label: 'قيد الانتظار', class: 'pending', icon: 'fa-clock' },
        'in_progress': { label: 'جاري التفريغ', class: 'in_progress', icon: 'fa-spinner' },
        'completed': { label: 'مكتمل', class: 'completed', icon: 'fa-circle-check' },
        'rejected': { label: 'مرفوض', class: 'rejected', icon: 'fa-circle-xmark' },
        'cancelled': { label: 'ملغى', class: 'cancelled', icon: 'fa-ban' }
    };
    const LAB_LABELS = { 'pending': '⏳ في الانتظار', 'passed': '✅ مقبول', 'failed': '❌ مرفوض', 'warning': '⚠️ تحذير' };

    const state = {
        rows: [], filtered: [], editing: null, editingId: 0, filter: 'all', loadedAt: null,
        lookups: { suppliers: [], tanks: [], fuelTypes: [], currencies: [], cashBoxes: [] },
        aiInsights: [], nextRefillCode: '',
        supplierBalances: {},
        currentUserName: '',
        lastSavedRow: null
    };

    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
    const bridge = window.AndroidInterface;
    const esc = (v) => String(v == null ? '—' : v).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
    const num = (v) => { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0; };
    const fmt = (v, dec=2) => num(v).toLocaleString('ar-EG', {minimumFractionDigits: dec, maximumFractionDigits: dec});
    const fmtShort = (v) => {
        const n = num(v);
        if (n >= 1_000_000) return (n/1_000_000).toFixed(1) + 'M';
        if (n >= 1000) return (n/1000).toFixed(1) + 'K';
        return n.toLocaleString('en');
    };
    const today = () => {
        const d = new Date(); const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
    };
    const nowLocal = () => {
        const d = new Date(); const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}T${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
    };
    const nowReadable = () => {
        try {
            return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ar-EG', {
                year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit',
                hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false
            }).format(new Date());
        } catch (_) {
            const d = new Date(); const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2,'0');
            return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
        }
    };

    function toast(message, kind='info', ms=3800) {
        const node = $('toast'); const icon = $('toastIcon'); const msg = $('toastMessage');
        msg.textContent = message; node.className = `toast show ${kind}`;
        icon.className = `fas ${kind === 'success' ? 'fa-check-circle' : kind === 'error' ? 'fa-times-circle' : kind === 'warning' ? 'fa-exclamation-triangle' : 'fa-info-circle'}`;
        clearTimeout(node._timer); node._timer = setTimeout(() => node.classList.remove('show'), ms);
    }
    function setLoading(on) { $('loadingIndicator').classList.toggle('show', Boolean(on)); }
    function parse(raw) {
        if (raw == null) throw new Error('لم يرجع الجسر نتيجة');
        const v = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
        if (v && v.success === false) throw new Error(v.error || v.message || 'فشلت العملية');
        return v;
    }
    function dataOf(v) { return v && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(v,'data') ? v.data : v; }
    function invoke(method, args=[]) {
        if (!bridge || typeof bridge[method] !== 'function') return Promise.reject(new Error(`العقد ${method} غير متاح`));
        try { return Promise.resolve(parse(bridge[method](...args))); }
        catch (e) { return Promise.reject(e); }
    }
    function rowsFrom(v) {
        const raw = dataOf(v);
        if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw;
        if (raw && Array.isArray(raw.rows)) return raw.rows;
        if (raw && Array.isArray(raw.data)) return raw.data;
        if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') return [raw];
        return [];
    }
    function field(row, names) {
        for (const n of (names || [])) {
            if (row && row[n] !== undefined && row[n] !== null && row[n] !== '') return row[n];
        }
        return '';
    }

    function getPaymentMethod(row) {
        let method = '';
        try {
            if (row.extra_data) {
                const extra = typeof row.extra_data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.extra_data) : row.extra_data;
                method = String(extra.payment_method || '').toLowerCase();
            }
        } catch (_) {}
        if (!method) method = String(row.payment_method || '').toLowerCase();
        return method === 'credit' ? 'credit' : 'cash';
    }
    function getCashBoxId(row) {
        try {
            if (row.extra_data) {
                const extra = typeof row.extra_data === 'string' ? JSON.parse(row.extra_data) : row.extra_data;
                const id = Number(extra.cash_box_id || 0);
                if (id > 0) return id;
            }
        } catch (_) {}
        return Number(row.cash_box_id || 0);
    }

    // ============ Theme ============
    function applyTheme() {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light';
        if (saved === 'dark') document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
        else document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme');
        const icon = $('themeIcon');
        if (icon) icon.className = saved === 'dark' ? 'fas fa-sun' : 'fas fa-moon';
    }
    function toggleTheme() {
        const isDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
        if (isDark) { document.documentElement.removeAttribute('data-theme'); localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light'); $('themeIcon').className = 'fas fa-moon'; }
        else { document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark'); localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark'); $('themeIcon').className = 'fas fa-sun'; }
    }

    // ============ Lookups ============
    async function loadLookups() {
        const tasks = [
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getSuppliers === 'function') ? invoke('getSuppliers',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getTanks === 'function') ? invoke('getTanks',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getFuelTypes === 'function') ? invoke('getFuelTypes',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getCurrencies === 'function') ? invoke('getCurrencies',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getNextRefillCode === 'function') ? invoke('getNextRefillCode',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getActiveCashBoxes === 'function') ? invoke('getActiveCashBoxes',[]) : Promise.resolve(null),
            (bridge && typeof bridge.getCurrentUser === 'function') ? invoke('getCurrentUser',[]) : Promise.resolve(null)
        ];
        const [sup, tanks, fuels, curr, nextCode, cashBoxes, user] = await Promise.all(tasks.map(p => p.catch(() => null)));
        state.lookups.suppliers = rowsFrom(sup);
        state.lookups.tanks = rowsFrom(tanks);
        state.lookups.fuelTypes = rowsFrom(fuels);
        state.lookups.currencies = rowsFrom(curr);
        state.lookups.cashBoxes = rowsFrom(cashBoxes);
        const nc = dataOf(nextCode);
        state.nextRefillCode = (nc && nc.refill_code) ? nc.refill_code : '';
        const u = dataOf(user);
        if (u) {
            state.currentUserName = u.full_name_ar || u.full_name || u.display_name || u.username || '';
        }
    }

    async function loadSupplierBalance(supplierId) {
        if (!supplierId || Number(supplierId) <= 0) return null;
        if (state.supplierBalances[supplierId] !== undefined) return state.supplierBalances[supplierId];
        try {
            const r = await invoke('getSupplierOutstandingBalance', [Number(supplierId)]);
            const d = dataOf(r) || {};
            state.supplierBalances[supplierId] = Number(d.current_balance || 0);
            return state.supplierBalances[supplierId];
        } catch (_) { return null; }
    }

    // ============ Load Data ============
    function renderSkeleton() {
        $('cardsContainer').innerHTML = Array.from({length: 4}).map(() => `
            <div class="skeleton-card"><div class="skeleton-row long"></div><div class="skeleton-row medium"></div><div class="skeleton-row short"></div></div>
        `).join('');
    }

    async function reloadData() {
        if (!bridge || typeof bridge.getTankRefillRecords !== 'function') {
            $('offlineBanner').classList.add('show'); renderSkeleton(); setTimeout(renderCards, 400); return;
        }
        setLoading(true);
        try {
            const params = { limit: 300, offset: 0, search: ($('searchInput').value || '').trim() };
            if (state.filter === 'pending' || state.filter === 'completed') params.status = state.filter;
            const result = await invoke('getTankRefillRecords', [JSON.stringify(params)]);
            state.rows = rowsFrom(result);
            state.loadedAt = new Date();
            $('offlineBanner').classList.remove('show');
            applyFilter();
        } catch (e) {
            state.rows = []; state.loadedAt = new Date(); renderCards(); toast(e.message || 'فشل التحميل', 'error');
        } finally { setLoading(false); }
    }

    function handleSearch() { clearTimeout(window._searchTimer); window._searchTimer = setTimeout(applyFilter, 250); }
    function setFilter(filter, btn) {
        state.filter = filter;
        document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
        if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');
        applyFilter();
    }
    function applyFilter() {
        const q = ($('searchInput').value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        state.filtered = state.rows.filter(row => {
            if (state.filter === 'cash' || state.filter === 'credit') {
                if (getPaymentMethod(row) !== state.filter) return false;
            } else if (state.filter !== 'all') {
                if (String(row.status || '') !== state.filter) return false;
            }
            if (!q) return true;
            return JSON.stringify(row || {}).toLowerCase().includes(q);
        });
        renderStats(); renderCards();
    }

    function renderStats() {
        const rows = state.filtered;
        $('statTotal').textContent = rows.length.toLocaleString('ar-EG');
        $('supplyCount').textContent = rows.length.toLocaleString('ar-EG');

        let cashTotal = 0, creditTotal = 0, litersTotal = 0;
        rows.forEach(r => {
            const net = num(field(r, ['net_amount','total_amount']));
            const liters = num(field(r, ['actual_quantity','delivered_quantity']));
            litersTotal += liters;
            if (getPaymentMethod(r) === 'credit') creditTotal += net; else cashTotal += net;
        });
        $('statCash').textContent = cashTotal ? fmtShort(cashTotal) : '0';
        $('statCredit').textContent = creditTotal ? fmtShort(creditTotal) : '0';
        $('statLiters').textContent = litersTotal ? fmtShort(litersTotal) : '0';
        $('statSupplierDebt').textContent = creditTotal ? fmtShort(creditTotal) : '0';
    }

    function statusBadge(status) {
        const key = String(status || 'pending').toLowerCase();
        const info = STATUS_LABELS[key] || STATUS_LABELS.pending;
        return `<span class="status-badge ${info.class}"><i class="fas ${info.icon}"></i> ${info.label}</span>`;
    }
    function paymentBadge(method) {
        if (method === 'credit') return `<span class="payment-badge credit"><i class="fas fa-file-invoice-dollar"></i> آجل</span>`;
        return `<span class="payment-badge cash"><i class="fas fa-money-bill-wave"></i> نقد</span>`;
    }
    function qualityBadge(result) {
        const key = String(result || 'pending').toLowerCase();
        const map = {
            'pending': { bg: 'rgba(217,119,6,0.15)', color: '#d97706', label: 'فحص معلق', icon: 'fa-flask' },
            'passed': { bg: 'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)', color: '#10b981', label: 'مقبول', icon: 'fa-circle-check' },
            'failed': { bg: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.15)', color: '#ef4444', label: 'مرفوض', icon: 'fa-circle-xmark' },
            'warning': { bg: 'rgba(217,119,6,0.15)', color: '#d97706', label: 'تحذير', icon: 'fa-triangle-exclamation' }
        };
        const info = map[key] || map.pending;
        return `<span style="background:${info.bg};color:${info.color};padding:3px 10px;border-radius:10px;font-size:0.66rem;font-weight:700;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;"><i class="fas ${info.icon}"></i> ${info.label}</span>`;
    }

    function renderCards() {
        const box = $('cardsContainer');
        if (!state.filtered.length) {
            box.innerHTML = `<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-inbox"></i><p style="font-weight:700;">لا توجد فواتير مطابقة</p><p style="font-size:0.78rem;">اضغط <strong>+</strong> لإضافة فاتورة توريد.</p></div>`;
            return;
        }

        box.innerHTML = state.filtered.map((row, idx) => {
            const id = esc(field(row, ['id']) || idx);
            const refCode = field(row, ['refill_code']) || '—';
            const supplier = field(row, ['supplier_name','supplier_id']) || '—';
            const tankName = field(row, ['tank_name','tank_id']) || '—';
            const fuelName = field(row, ['fuel_name_ar','fuel_name','fuel_type_id']) || '—';
            const delivered = num(field(row, ['delivered_quantity']));
            const actual = num(field(row, ['actual_quantity'])) || delivered;
            const netAmount = num(field(row, ['net_amount','total_amount']));
            const tankerNumber = field(row, ['tanker_number']) || '—';
            const tankerDriver = field(row, ['tanker_driver']) || '—';
            const orderDate = String(field(row, ['order_date','arrival_date','created_at']) || '—').slice(0, 10);
            const status = field(row, ['status']) || 'pending';
            const labResult = field(row, ['lab_test_result']) || 'pending';
            const paymentMethod = getPaymentMethod(row);

            return `
                <article class="supply-card" data-id="${id}" onclick="selectCard(this, ${id})" style="animation-delay:${idx * 0.03}s;">
                    <div class="card-header">
                        <div style="text-align:right;flex:1;min-width:0;">
                            <div class="invoice-chip"><i class="fas fa-file-invoice"></i> ${esc(refCode)}</div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="card-header-right">
                            ${paymentBadge(paymentMethod)}
                            ${statusBadge(status)}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="supplier-name"><i class="fas fa-user-tie"></i> ${esc(supplier)}</div>
                    <div class="fuel-badge"><i class="fas fa-gas-pump"></i> ${esc(fuelName)}</div>

                    <div class="metrics-grid">
                        <div class="metric-box"><div class="metric-value">${fmtShort(delivered)} ل</div><div class="metric-label">الكمية الموردة</div></div>
                        <div class="metric-box ${paymentMethod === 'credit' ? 'credit-metric' : 'cash-metric'}">
                            <div class="metric-value">${fmtShort(netAmount)}</div>
                            <div class="metric-label">${paymentMethod === 'credit' ? 'قيمة آجلة' : 'قيمة نقدية'}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="card-info-grid">
                        <div class="card-info-item"><span class="label"><i class="fas fa-database"></i> الخزان</span><span class="value">${esc(tankName)}</span></div>
                        <div class="card-info-item"><span class="label"><i class="fas fa-truck"></i> الصهريج</span><span class="value">${esc(tankerNumber)}</span></div>
                        <div class="card-info-item"><span class="label"><i class="fas fa-user"></i> السائق</span><span class="value">${esc(tankerDriver)}</span></div>
                        <div class="card-info-item"><span class="label"><i class="fas fa-calendar"></i> التاريخ</span><span class="value">${esc(orderDate)}</span></div>
                    </div>

                    <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin:8px 0 4px;">
                        <span style="font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--text-muted);"><i class="fas fa-vial"></i> حالة الجودة</span>
                        ${qualityBadge(labResult)}
                    </div>

                    <div class="card-actions" onclick="event.stopPropagation();">
                        <button class="card-btn" type="button" onclick="viewDetails(${id})"><i class="fas fa-eye"></i> تفاصيل</button>
                        <button class="card-btn" type="button" onclick="editSupply(${id})"><i class="fas fa-edit"></i> تعديل</button>
                        <button class="card-btn" type="button" onclick="viewInvoice(${id})"><i class="fas fa-file-invoice"></i> فاتورة</button>
                        ${status !== 'completed' ? `<button class="card-btn success" type="button" onclick="approveSupply(${id})"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> اعتماد</button>` : ''}
                        <button class="card-btn danger" type="button" onclick="deleteSupply(${id})"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button>
                    </div>
                </article>
            `;
        }).join('');
    }

    function selectCard(card, id) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.supply-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('selected'));
        if (card) card.classList.add('selected');
        state.selectedId = id;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // FORM
    // ============================================================
    function selectOptions(items, valueKey, labelKey, placeholder='اختر...') {
        return `<option value="">${placeholder}</option>` + items.map(item => {
            const value = field(item, [valueKey]);
            const label = field(item, [labelKey]) || value;
            return `<option value="${esc(value)}">${esc(label)}</option>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    function buildFormHtml(row) {

        const cashBoxId = row ? getCashBoxId(row) : '';
        // تاريخ ووقت الطلب يُدخلان آلياً عند الإنشاء
        const orderDateTime = row ? (get('arrival_date') || nowLocal()) : nowLocal();

        return `
            <!-- ===== رقم الفاتورة والطلب ===== -->
            <div class="form-section-title"><i class="fas fa-file-invoice"></i> بيانات الفاتورة الأساسية</div>

                <label class="form-label">رقم فاتورة التوريد (آلي) <span class="req">*</span></label>

            </div>
            <div class="form-group">
                <label class="form-label">رقم فاتورة المورد</label>
                <input class="form-input" type="text" data-key="invoice_number" value="${esc(get('invoice_number'))}" placeholder="رقم فاتورة المورد">
            </div>
            <div class="form-group">

            <div class="form-group">

                <select class="form-input" data-key="status" required>
                    ${Object.entries(STATUS_LABELS).map(([k,v]) => `<option value="${k}" ${defaultStatus === k ? 'selected' : ''}>${v.label}</option>`).join('')}

            <div class="form-group">
