<script>
(()=>{'use strict';
const S={w:[],p:[],t:[],ps:[],pd:[],pc:null,fs:[],fd:[],fc:null,mode:'p',report:[]},
$=id=>document.getElementById(id),
N=v=>Number(v)||0,
D=x=>x&&x.data!==undefined?x.data:x,
R=x=>{const v=D(x);return Array.isArray(v)?v:(v?.rows||[])},
esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c])),
call=(n,a=[])=>{try{if(!AndroidInterface||typeof AndroidInterface[n]!=='function')throw Error('الدالة '+n+' غير متاحة');let x=AndroidInterface[n](...a);x=typeof x==='string'?JSON.parse(x):x;if(x?.success===false)throw Error(x.error||x.message||'فشلت العملية');return Promise.resolve(x)}catch(e){return Promise.reject(e)}},
busy=x=>$('busy').classList.toggle('show',x),
toast=(x,e)=>{$('toast').textContent=x;$('toast').className='toast show'+(e?' error':'');clearTimeout(window._t);window._t=setTimeout(()=>$('toast').className='toast',3500)},
closeM=id=>$(id).classList.remove('show'),
wh=id=>S.w.find(x=>N(x.id||x.warehouse_id)===N(id))?.warehouse_name||S.w.find(x=>N(x.id||x.warehouse_id)===N(id))?.name||'مستودع',
tank=id=>S.t.find(x=>N(x.id||x.tank_id)===N(id)),
status=s=>s==='completed'?'معتمد':s==='in_progress'?'قيد الجرد':'مسودة',
dateOnly=v=>String(v||'').slice(0,10),
inRange=(v,f,t)=>{const d=dateOnly(v);return (!f||d>=f)&&(!t||d<=t)},
fmt=v=>N(v).toLocaleString('ar',{maximumFractionDigits:3}),
money=v=>N(v).toLocaleString('ar',{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}),
setTab=m=>{
 S.mode=m;
 $('tp').classList.toggle('active',m==='p');$('tf').classList.toggle('active',m==='f');$('tr').classList.toggle('active',m==='r');
 $('products').style.display=m==='p'?'block':'none';$('fuel').style.display=m==='f'?'block':'none';$('reports').style.display=m==='r'?'block':'none';
 if(m==='r')loadReport();
};
async function look(){S.w=R(await call('getWarehouses',[JSON.stringify({})]));S.p=R(await call('getProducts'));S.t=R(await call('getInventoryFuelTanks'));
 $('statProducts').textContent=S.p.length.toLocaleString('ar');$('statFuel').textContent=S.t.length.toLocaleString('ar');
 $('warehouse').innerHTML='<option value="">اختر المستودع</option>'+S.w.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.id||x.warehouse_id)}">${esc(x.warehouse_name||x.name)}</option>`).join('');
 $('product').innerHTML='<option value="">اختر المنتج</option>'+S.p.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.id||x.product_id)}">${esc(x.product_name||x.product_name_ar||x.name)}</option>`).join('');
 $('tank').innerHTML='<option value="">اختر الخزان</option>'+S.t.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.id||x.tank_id)}">${esc(x.tank_name||x.tank_code)} — ${esc(x.fuel_name_ar||x.fuel_name||'')}</option>`).join('');
}
async function loadP(){S.ps=R(await call('getStocktakeRecords',[JSON.stringify({limit:100})]));$('ps').innerHTML=S.ps.length?S.ps.map(x=>`<button class="session ${S.pc&&N(S.pc.id)===N(x.id)?'active':''}" onclick="selP(${N(x.id)})"><strong>📦 ${esc(wh(x.warehouse_id))}</strong><small>${esc(x.start_date||x.created_at||'')} · <span class="badge ${esc(x.status||'draft')}">${status(x.status)}</span></small></button>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">لا توجد جلسات.</div>'}
async function loadF(){S.fs=R(await call('getFuelStocktakeRecords',[JSON.stringify({limit:100})]));$('fs').innerHTML=S.fs.length?S.fs.map(x=>`<button class="session ${S.fc&&N(S.fc.id)===N(x.id)?'active':''}" onclick="selF(${N(x.id)})"><strong>⛽ جرد وقود #${N(x.id)}</strong><small>${esc(x.start_date||x.created_at||'')} · ${N(x.line_count)} خزانات · <span class="badge ${esc(x.status||'draft')}">${status(x.status)}</span></small></button>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">لا توجد جلسات جرد وقود.</div>'}
function metrics(a,f){const q=a.reduce((s,x)=>s+N(x[f?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']),0),v=a.reduce((s,x)=>s+N(x.variance_value),0),c=a.filter(x=>Math.abs(N(f?x.variance_quantity:x.quantity_variance))>0).length;return `<div class="metrics"><div class="metric"><b>${a.length.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>بنود</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${fmt(q)}</b><span>${f?'فرق اللترات':'فرق الكمية'}</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${money(v)}</b><span>فرق القيمة</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${c.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>بنود بها فروقات</span></div></div>`}
function cards(a,f,editable){return a.length?a.map(x=>{const q=N(f?x.variance_quantity:x.quantity_variance),v=N(x.variance_value),name=f?(x.tank_name||x.tank_code):(x.product_name||x.product_name_ar);return `<article class="item"><h3>${f?'⛽':'📦'} ${esc(name)}</h3><div class="grid"><div class="cell"><span>النظام</span><b>${fmt(x.system_quantity)}</b></div><div class="cell"><span>العد</span><b>${fmt(x.counted_quantity)}</b></div><div class="cell"><span>الفرق</span><b class="${q<0?'minus':q>0?'plus':''}">${q>0?'+':''}${fmt(q)}</b></div><div class="cell"><span>فرق القيمة</span><b>${money(v)}</b></div><div class="cell"><span>ملاحظات</span><b>${esc(x.notes||'—')}</b></div></div>${editable?`<button class="btn light" onclick="${f?'openFL':'openPL'}(${N(x.detail_id)})">إعادة عد</button>`:''}</article>`}).join(''):'<div class="empty">لم يسجل أي عدّ بعد.</div>'}
async function selP(id){S.pc=S.ps.find(x=>N(x.id)===N(id));busy(true);try{S.pd=R(await call('getStocktakeDetails',[id]));renderP()}catch(e){toast(e.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function renderP(){const s=S.pc,ed=s&&(s.status==='draft'||s.status==='in_progress');if(!s)return;$('pd').innerHTML=`<div class="head"><div><h2>📦 ${esc(wh(s.warehouse_id))}</h2><p class="muted">الحالة: ${status(s.status)}</p></div><div class="actions"><button class="btn light" ${ed?'':'disabled'} onclick="openPL()">تسجيل عدّ</button><button class="btn success" ${ed&&S.pd.length?'':'disabled'} onclick="approveP()">اعتماد</button></div></div>${metrics(S.pd,false)}<div class="notice">${ed?'يمكن إعادة العد قبل الاعتماد. الاعتماد ينشئ حركة adjustment حقيقية.':'سجل معتمد وغير قابل للتعديل.'}</div><div class="table"><table><thead><tr><th>المنتج</th><th>رصيد النظام</th><th>العد الفعلي</th><th>الفرق</th><th>فرق القيمة</th><th>ملاحظات</th><th>إجراء</th></tr></thead><tbody>${S.pd.map(x=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.product_name||x.product_name_ar)}</td><td class="num">${fmt(x.system_quantity)}</td><td class="num">${fmt(x.counted_quantity)}</td><td class="num ${N(x.quantity_variance)<0?'minus':N(x.quantity_variance)>0?'plus':''}">${fmt(x.quantity_variance)}</td><td class="num">${money(x.variance_value)}</td><td>${esc(x.notes||'—')}</td><td>${ed?`<button class="btn light" onclick="openPL(${N(x.detail_id)})">تعديل</button>`:'موثق'}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="7" class="empty">لا توجد بنود.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div><div class="cards">${cards(S.pd,false,ed)}</div>`;loadP()}
async function selF(id){S.fc=S.fs.find(x=>N(x.id)===N(id));busy(true);try{S.fd=R(await call('getFuelStocktakeDetails',[id]));renderF()}catch(e){toast(e.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function renderF(){const s=S.fc,ed=s&&(s.status==='draft'||s.status==='in_progress');if(!s)return;$('fd').innerHTML=`<div class="head"><div><h2>⛽ جرد وقود #${N(s.id)}</h2><p class="muted">الحالة: ${status(s.status)}</p></div><div class="actions"><button class="btn light" ${ed?'':'disabled'} onclick="openFL()">تسجيل خزان</button><button class="btn success" ${ed&&S.fd.length?'':'disabled'} onclick="approveF()">اعتماد التسوية</button></div></div>${metrics(S.fd,true)}<div class="notice">فرق الوقود يحسب مقابل رصيد الخزان. الاعتماد يسجل حركة fuel stocktake adjustment ويحدث رصيد الخزان داخل معاملة.</div><div class="table"><table><thead><tr><th>الخزان</th><th>الوقود</th><th>رصيد النظام</th><th>العد الفعلي</th><th>الفرق لتر</th><th>فرق القيمة</th><th>ملاحظات</th><th>إجراء</th></tr></thead><tbody>${S.fd.map(x=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.tank_name||x.tank_code)}</td><td>${esc(x.fuel_name||'')}</td><td class="num">${fmt(x.system_quantity)}</td><td class="num">${fmt(x.counted_quantity)}</td><td class="num ${N(x.variance_quantity)<0?'minus':N(x.variance_quantity)>0?'plus':''}">${fmt(x.variance_quantity)}</td><td class="num">${money(x.variance_value)}</td><td>${esc(x.notes||'—')}</td><td>${ed?`<button class="btn light" onclick="openFL(${N(x.detail_id)})">تعديل</button>`:'موثق'}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty">لا توجد بنود.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div><div class="cards">${cards(S.fd,true,ed)}</div>`;loadF()}
function newProductSession(){$('pf').reset();$('pm').classList.add('show')}
async function saveP(e){e.preventDefault();busy(true);try{const o=await call('saveStocktakeRecord',[JSON.stringify({warehouse_id:N($('warehouse').value),status:'draft',notes:$('pn').value})]);closeM('pm');await loadP();await selP(N(D(o)?.id||o.id))}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function openPL(id=''){if(!S.pc)return;$('plf').reset();$('pid').value=id||'';const x=S.pd.find(d=>N(d.detail_id)===N(id));if(x){$('product').value=x.product_id;$('pc').value=x.counted_quantity;$('pnotes').value=x.notes||'';$('psys').textContent=`رصيد النظام عند التسجيل: ${fmt(x.system_quantity)} — سيعاد احتساب الفرق من SQLite عند الحفظ.`}else $('psys').textContent='سيتم التقاط رصيد النظام من SQLite عند الحفظ.';$('plm').classList.add('show')}
async function savePL(e){e.preventDefault();busy(true);try{const p={stocktake_id:N(S.pc.id),product_id:N($('product').value),counted_quantity:N($('pc').value),notes:$('pnotes').value};if(N($('pid').value))await call('updateStocktakeDetailRecord',[N($('pid').value),JSON.stringify(p)]);else await call('saveStocktakeDetailRecord',[JSON.stringify(p)]);closeM('plm');await selP(S.pc.id);toast('تم حفظ عد المنتج')}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
async function approveP(){if(!confirm('اعتماد فروقات المنتجات وإنشاء التسويات؟'))return;busy(true);try{await call('resolveStocktakeRecord',[N(S.pc.id),'اعتماد']);await loadP();await selP(S.pc.id);toast('تم اعتماد جرد المنتجات')}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function newFuelSession(){$('ff').reset();$('fm').classList.add('show')}
async function saveF(e){e.preventDefault();busy(true);try{const o=await call('saveFuelStocktakeRecord',[JSON.stringify({status:'draft',notes:$('fn').value})]);closeM('fm');await loadF();await selF(N(D(o)?.id||o.id))}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function openFL(id=''){if(!S.fc)return;$('flf').reset();$('fid').value=id||'';const x=S.fd.find(d=>N(d.detail_id)===N(id));if(x){$('tank').value=x.tank_id;$('fc').value=x.counted_quantity;$('fnotes').value=x.notes||'';$('fsys').textContent=`رصيد النظام عند التسجيل: ${fmt(x.system_quantity)} — سيعاد احتساب الفرق من SQLite عند الحفظ.`}else $('fsys').textContent='سيتم التقاط رصيد الخزان من SQLite عند الحفظ.';$('flm').classList.add('show')}
async function saveFL(e){e.preventDefault();busy(true);try{const t=tank($('tank').value),p={fuel_stocktake_id:N(S.fc.id),tank_id:N($('tank').value),fuel_type_id:N(t?.fuel_type_id),counted_quantity:N($('fc').value),notes:$('fnotes').value};if(N($('fid').value))await call('updateFuelStocktakeDetailRecord',[N($('fid').value),JSON.stringify(p)]);else await call('saveFuelStocktakeDetailRecord',[JSON.stringify(p)]);closeM('flm');await selF(S.fc.id);toast('تم حفظ عد الوقود')}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
async function approveF(){if(!confirm('اعتماد فروقات الوقود وتحديث أرصدة الخزانات؟'))return;busy(true);try{await call('resolveFuelStocktakeRecord',[N(S.fc.id),'اعتماد']);await loadF();await selF(S.fc.id);toast('تم اعتماد جرد الوقود')}catch(x){toast(x.message,true)}finally{busy(false)}}
function reportParams(){return {limit:1000,start_date:$('rf').value,end_date:$('rt').value,status:$('rs').value==='all'?'':$('rs').value}}
async function loadReport(){
 busy(true);
 try{
   const k=$('rk').value, f=$('rf').value, t=$('rt').value, q=$('rq').value.trim().toLowerCase(), st=$('rs').value;
   const rows=[];
   if(k==='all'||k==='product'){
     const rep=D(await call('generateStocktakeReport',[JSON.stringify(reportParams())]));
     const productSessions=R(rep);
     for(const x of productSessions){
       const d=dateOnly(x.start_date||x.created_at);
       if(!inRange(d,f,t)||(st!=='all'&&x.status!==st))continue;
       const details=R(await call('getStocktakeDetails',[N(x.id)]));
       const warehouse=wh(x.warehouse_id);
       const searchable=`${warehouse} ${x.id} ${x.notes||''} ${details.map(z=>z.product_name||z.product_name_ar||'').join(' ')}`.toLowerCase();
       if(q&&!searchable.includes(q))continue;
       rows.push({kind:'product',id:N(x.id),date:d,status:x.status||'draft',title:`جرد منتجات #${N(x.id)}`,subtitle:warehouse,notes:x.notes||'',details});
     }
   }
   if(k==='all'||k==='fuel'){
     const fuelSessions=R(await call('getFuelStocktakeRecords',[JSON.stringify({limit:1000})]));
     for(const x of fuelSessions){
       const d=dateOnly(x.start_date||x.created_at);
       if(!inRange(d,f,t)||(st!=='all'&&x.status!==st))continue;
       const details=R(await call('getFuelStocktakeDetails',[N(x.id)]));
       const searchable=`جرد وقود ${x.id} ${x.notes||''} ${details.map(z=>z.tank_name||z.tank_code||'').join(' ')}`.toLowerCase();
       if(q&&!searchable.includes(q))continue;
       rows.push({kind:'fuel',id:N(x.id),date:d,status:x.status||'draft',title:`جرد وقود #${N(x.id)}`,subtitle:'الخزانات',notes:x.notes||'',details});
     }
   }
   rows.sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date))||b.id-a.id);S.report=rows;renderReport();
 }catch(e){$('reportRows').innerHTML=`<div class="report-empty">تعذر إنشاء التقرير: ${esc(e.message)}</div>`;toast(e.message,true)}
 finally{busy(false)}
}
function reportTotals(){
 const all=S.report.flatMap(r=>r.details.map(d=>({r,d}))), varianceQty=all.reduce((s,o)=>s+N(o.d[o.r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']),0), value=all.reduce((s,o)=>s+N(o.d.variance_value),0), diff=all.filter(o=>Math.abs(N(o.d[o.r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']))>0).length;
 return {sessions:S.report.length,lines:all.length,varianceQty,value,diff,approved:S.report.filter(x=>x.status==='completed').length};
}
function renderReport(){
 const t=reportTotals();
 $('reportSummary').innerHTML=`<div class="metric"><b>${t.sessions.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>جلسات الجرد</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${t.lines.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>بنود العد</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${fmt(t.varianceQty)}</b><span>صافي فرق الكمية/اللترات</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${money(t.value)}</b><span>صافي فرق القيمة</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${t.diff.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>بنود بها فروقات</span></div><div class="metric"><b>${t.approved.toLocaleString('ar')}</b><span>جلسات معتمدة</span></div>`;
 $('reportNotice').textContent=`يغطي التقرير ${t.sessions.toLocaleString('ar')} جلسة و${t.lines.toLocaleString('ar')} بند عد. الفروقات معروضة قبل/بعد الاعتماد كما هي مسجلة في SQLite.`;
 $('reportRows').innerHTML=S.report.length?S.report.map(r=>{
   const q=r.details.reduce((s,x)=>s+N(x[r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']),0),v=r.details.reduce((s,x)=>s+N(x.variance_value),0),c=r.details.filter(x=>Math.abs(N(x[r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']))>0).length;
   return `<article class="report-card"><div class="report-title"><div><strong>${r.kind==='fuel'?'⛽':'📦'} ${esc(r.title)}</strong><div class="muted">${esc(r.subtitle)} · ${esc(r.date||'—')}</div></div><span class="badge ${esc(r.status)}">${status(r.status)}</span></div><div class="report-meta"><div><span>البنود</span><b>${r.details.length.toLocaleString('ar')}</b></div><div><span>بنود بفروقات</span><b>${c.toLocaleString('ar')}</b></div><div><span>صافي الفرق</span><b>${fmt(q)}</b></div><div><span>فرق القيمة</span><b>${money(v)}</b></div></div><div class="variance">${r.details.length? r.details.slice(0,12).map(x=>{const qx=N(x[r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance']);const name=r.kind==='fuel'?(x.tank_name||x.tank_code):(x.product_name||x.product_name_ar);return `<div><b>${esc(name)}</b>: النظام ${fmt(x.system_quantity)} ← العد ${fmt(x.counted_quantity)} ← الفرق <span class="${qx<0?'minus':qx>0?'plus':''}">${fmt(qx)}</span></div>`}).join('')+(r.details.length>12?`<div class="muted">+ ${r.details.length-12} بنود أخرى</div>`:''):'لا توجد تفاصيل عد.'}</div>${r.notes?`<div class="muted" style="margin-top:6px">ملاحظات: ${esc(r.notes)}</div>`:''}</article>`;
 }).join(''):'<div class="report-empty">لا توجد نتائج وفق المرشحات الحالية.</div>';
}
function reportCsvRows(){
 const rows=[['النوع','رقم الجرد','التاريخ','الحالة','الموقع','العنصر','رصيد النظام','العد الفعلي','الفرق','فرق القيمة','ملاحظات']];
 S.report.forEach(r=>r.details.forEach(x=>rows.push([r.kind==='fuel'?'وقود':'منتجات',r.id,r.date,status(r.status),r.subtitle,r.kind==='fuel'?(x.tank_name||x.tank_code):(x.product_name||x.product_name_ar),x.system_quantity,x.counted_quantity,x[r.kind==='fuel'?'variance_quantity':'quantity_variance'],x.variance_value,x.notes||r.notes||''])));
 return rows;
}
function downloadCsv(rows,name){const c='\ufeff'+rows.map(r=>r.map(v=>`"${String(v??'').replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',')).join('\n'),u=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([c],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=u;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(u),500)}
function exportReportCsv(){if(!S.report.length)return toast('لا توجد بيانات للتصدير',true);downloadCsv(reportCsvRows(),'stocktake-reconciliation-report.csv')}
function printReport(){if(!S.report.length)return toast('قم بتحديث التقرير أولاً',true);setTab('r');setTimeout(()=>window.print(),80)}
function openReports(){setTab('r')}
function reloadFuel(){busy(true);look().then(loadF).then(()=>S.fc?selF(S.fc.id):null).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true)).finally(()=>busy(false))}
function reload(){busy(true);Promise.all([look(),loadP(),loadF()]).catch(e=>toast(e.message,true)).finally(()=>busy(false))}
function exportCsv(){const a=S.mode==='f'?S.fd:S.pd;if(!a.length)return toast('اختر جلسة تحتوي على بيانات',true);const h=S.mode==='f'?['الخزان','الوقود','النظام','العد','الفرق','القيمة']:['المنتج','النظام','العد','الفرق','القيمة'];const b=a.map(x=>S.mode==='f'?[x.tank_name,x.fuel_name,x.system_quantity,x.counted_quantity,x.variance_quantity,x.variance_value]:[x.product_name,x.system_quantity,x.counted_quantity,x.quantity_variance,x.variance_value]);downloadCsv([h,...b],S.mode==='f'?'fuel-stocktake.csv':'product-stocktake.csv')}
function goHome(){window.location.href='../main.html'}
function theme(){const d=document.documentElement.classList.toggle('dark');$('themeI').className=d?'fas fa-sun':'fas fa-moon';localStorage.theme=d?'dark':'light'}
$('tp').onclick=()=>setTab('p');$('tf').onclick=()=>setTab('f');$('tr').onclick=()=>setTab('r');$('theme').onclick=theme;$('home').onclick=goHome;
$('pf').onsubmit=saveP;$('plf').onsubmit=savePL;$('ff').onsubmit=saveF;$('flf').onsubmit=saveFL;
['rf','rt','rk','rs','rq'].forEach(id=>$(id).addEventListener(id==='rq'?'input':'change',()=>{if(S.mode==='r')loadReport()}));
document.querySelectorAll('.modal').forEach(x=>x.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===x)closeM(x.id)});
Object.assign(window,{reload,newProductSession,openPL,approveP,newFuelSession,openFL,approveF,reloadFuel,closeM,exportCsv,openReports,loadReport,exportReportCsv,printReport,selP,selF});
reload();
})();
</script>
    <script src="file:///android_asset/assets-local/js/modal-interaction.js"></script>

    <script src="file:///android_asset/assets-local/js/export-runtime.js"></script>
</body></html>

            <option value="custom">مخصص (سيُطلب منك)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="modal-footer">
        <button type="button" class="btn primary" id="confirmSaveBtn"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> تأكيد الحفظ</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn" onclick="closeModal(null,'saveFileModal')"><i class="fas fa-times"></i> إلغاء</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toast" id="toast"></div>

  <script>
  (() => {
    'use strict';

    let source = [];
    let filter = 'open';
    let summary = {};
    let searchQuery = '';
    let searchTimer = null;
    let reportRows = [];

    const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
    const esc = v => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
    const n = v => Number.isFinite(Number(v)) ? Number(v) : 0;
    const data = x => x && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(x, 'data') ? x.data : x;

    /* ============================================================
       Bridge Wrapper — كما هو
       ============================================================ */
    function call(name, args = []) {
      if (!window.AndroidInterface || typeof window.AndroidInterface[name] !== 'function') {
        return Promise.reject(new Error('دالة ' + name + ' غير متاحة في هذا الإصدار'));
      }
      try {
        const raw = window.AndroidInterface[name](...args);
        const out = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
        if (out?.success === false) throw new Error(out.error || out.message || 'فشلت العملية');
        return Promise.resolve(out);
      } catch (e) {
        return Promise.reject(e);
      }
    }

    function rows(x) {
      const v = data(x);
      return Array.isArray(v) ? v : (Array.isArray(v?.rows) ? v.rows : (Array.isArray(v?.records) ? v.records : []));
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Busy / Toast
       ============================================================ */
    function busy(on) { const el = $('busy'); if (el) el.classList.toggle('show', !!on); }
    function toast(text, error = false) {
      const box = $('toast');
      if (!box) return;
      const cls = error ? 'error' : 'success';
      box.className = 'toast show ' + cls;
      box.innerHTML = '<span class="toast-icon"><i class="fas ' + (error ? 'fa-triangle-exclamation' : 'fa-circle-check') + '"></i></span><span>' + esc(text) + '</span>';
      clearTimeout(box._timer);
      box._timer = setTimeout(() => box.className = 'toast', 3800);
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Helpers
       ============================================================ */
    function level(row) {
      return row.alert_level === 'critical' || row.alert_type === 'out_of_stock' ? 'critical' : 'warning';
    }
    function levelLabel(row) {
      const l = level(row);
      if (Number(row.is_resolved) === 1) return 'معالج';
      return l === 'critical' ? 'حرج' : 'تحذير';
    }
    function alertTypeLabel(t) {
      const map = {
        out_of_stock: 'نفاد رصيد',
        low_stock: 'رصيد منخفض',
        critical: 'حرج',
        warning: 'تحذير',
        expired: 'منتهي الصلاحية',
        nearing_expiry: 'قارب الانتهاء',
        overstock: 'تكدس مخزون'
      };
      return map[t] || t || '—';
    }
    function itemTypeLabel(t) {
      if (t === 'fuel') return 'وقود';
      if (t === 'product') return 'منتج';
      return t || '—';
    }
    const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    function fmtNum(v) {
      const num = n(v);
      return num.toLocaleString('en');
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Apply Theme — كما في cashboxes
       ============================================================ */
    function applyTheme() {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light';
      if (saved === 'dark') document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'dark';
      const icon = $('themeIcon');
      if (icon) icon.className = saved === 'dark' ? 'fas fa-sun' : 'fas fa-moon';
    }
    function toggleTheme() {
      const isDark = document.documentElement.dataset.theme === 'dark';
      if (isDark) delete document.documentElement.dataset.theme;
      else document.documentElement.dataset.theme = 'dark';
      localStorage.setItem('theme', isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
      const icon = $('themeIcon');
      if (icon) icon.className = isDark ? 'fas fa-moon' : 'fas fa-sun';
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Filter Tabs — مرتبطة بمحتوى الشاشة
       ============================================================ */
    const FILTER_TABS = [
      { id: 'open',      label: 'غير محلولة', icon: 'fa-circle-exclamation' },
      { id: 'critical',  label: 'الحرجة',     icon: 'fa-triangle-exclamation' },
      { id: 'warning',   label: 'التحذيرية',  icon: 'fa-bell' },
      { id: 'resolved',  label: 'المعالجة',   icon: 'fa-circle-check' },
      { id: 'all',       label: 'الكل',       icon: 'fa-list' }
    ];

    function renderFilters() {
      const chips = $('filterChips');
      if (!chips) return;
      chips.innerHTML = FILTER_TABS.map(t => `
        <button type="button" class="filter-chip ${t.id === filter ? 'active' : ''}" data-filter="${t.id}">
          <i class="fas ${t.icon}"></i><span>${esc(t.label)}</span>
        </button>
      `).join('');
      chips.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          chips.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
          btn.classList.add('active');
          filter = btn.dataset.filter;
          render();
        });
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Stats
       ============================================================ */
    function renderStats() {
      const s = summary.statistics || summary;
      const total = n(s.total_count ?? summary.total_count);
      const unresolved = n(s.unresolved_count ?? summary.unresolved_count);
      const critical = n(s.critical_count ?? summary.critical_count);
      const resolved = Math.max(0, total - unresolved);
      if ($('statTotal')) $('statTotal').textContent = total.toLocaleString('en');
      if ($('statOpen')) $('statOpen').textContent = unresolved.toLocaleString('en');
      if ($('statCritical')) $('statCritical').textContent = critical.toLocaleString('en');
      if ($('statResolved')) $('statResolved').textContent = resolved.toLocaleString('en');
      if ($('itemCount')) $('itemCount').textContent = visible().length.toLocaleString('en');
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Visible — يعمل مع searchQuery أيضاً
       ============================================================ */
    function matchesSearch(a) {
      if (!searchQuery) return true;
      const hay = [
        a.product_name || '',
        a.alert_type || '',
        a.alert_level || '',
        a.item_type || '',
        a.resolution_notes || '',
        a.notes || '',
        String(a.product_id || ''),
        String(a.fuel_type_id || '')
      ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
      return hay.includes(searchQuery);
    }

    function visible() {
      return source.filter(a => {
        if (!matchesSearch(a)) return false;
        if (filter === 'all') return true;
        if (filter === 'critical') return level(a) === 'critical' && Number(a.is_resolved) !== 1;
        if (filter === 'warning') return level(a) === 'warning' && Number(a.is_resolved) !== 1;
        if (filter === 'resolved') return Number(a.is_resolved) === 1;
        // filter === 'open'
        return Number(a.is_resolved) !== 1;
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Render
       ============================================================ */
    function render() {
      renderStats();
      const list = visible();
      const box = $('alerts');
      if (!box) return;
      if (!list.length) {
        box.innerHTML = `
          <div class="empty-state" style="grid-column:1/-1;">
            <i class="fas fa-inbox"></i>
            <p>لا توجد تنبيهات مطابقة للفئة المحددة أو البحث.</p>
          </div>
        `;
        return;
      }
      box.innerHTML = list.map(a => {
        const lvl = level(a);
        const resolved = Number(a.is_resolved) === 1;
        const isFuel = a.item_type === 'fuel';
        const shortage = n(a.shortage_quantity);
        const state = resolved ? 'resolved' : lvl;
        const title = resolved ? 'تنبيه تمت معالجته' : (lvl === 'critical' ? 'نفاد رصيد المنتج' : 'رصيد أقل من حد التنبيه');
        const alertId = a.id || a.alert_id;
        const restockAttr = isFuel ? `data-fuel="${esc(a.fuel_type_id || '')}"` : `data-restock="${esc(a.product_id || '')}"`;
        const resolveButton = (!resolved && !isFuel)
          ? `<button type="button" class="btn warning" data-resolve="${esc(alertId)}"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> تسجيل المعالجة</button>`
          : '';

        const typeLabel = alertTypeLabel(a.alert_type);
        const itemLabel = itemTypeLabel(a.item_type);
        const resolvedAt = a.resolved_at ? `<span class="alert-note"><i class="fas fa-clock"></i> تم الحل في: ${esc(a.resolved_at)}</span>` : '';
        const notes = a.resolution_notes ? `<span class="alert-note"><i class="fas fa-note-sticky"></i> ${esc(a.resolution_notes)}</span>` : '';

        const primaryBtn = !resolved
          ? `<button type="button" class="btn primary" ${restockAttr}><i class="fas fa-plus"></i> إضافة رصيد</button>`
          : `<button type="button" class="btn" ${restockAttr}><i class="fas fa-history"></i> مراجعة الحركة</button>`;

        return `
          <article class="alert-card ${state}">
            <div class="alert-head">
              <div class="alert-title-wrap">
                <h3 class="alert-title">${esc(a.product_name || 'منتج')} — ${title}</h3>
                <small class="alert-meta">سجل #${esc(alertId || '')} · ${esc(a.created_at || '')}</small>
              </div>
              <span class="alert-badge ${state}">${levelLabel(a)}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="alert-details">
              <div class="alert-detail"><span class="label">الرصيد الحالي</span><b class="value">${fmtNum(a.current_quantity)}</b></div>
              <div class="alert-detail"><span class="label">حد التنبيه</span><b class="value">${fmtNum(a.threshold_quantity)}</b></div>
              <div class="alert-detail"><span class="label">العجز</span><b class="value">${fmtNum(shortage)}</b></div>
            </div>
            <div class="alert-details" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr)); margin-top:0;">
              <div class="alert-detail"><span class="label">نوع التنبيه</span><b class="value" style="font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;direction:rtl;">${esc(typeLabel)}</b></div>
              <div class="alert-detail"><span class="label">المصدر</span><b class="value" style="font-family:'Cairo',sans-serif;direction:rtl;">${esc(itemLabel)}</b></div>
            </div>
            ${isFuel ? '<span class="alert-note"><i class="fas fa-gas-pump"></i> مصدر التنبيه: رصيد الخزان الفعلي</span>' : ''}
            ${notes}
            ${resolvedAt}
            <div class="alert-actions">
              ${primaryBtn}
              ${resolveButton}
            </div>
          </article>
        `;
      }).join('');

      // ربط الأزرار — نفس السلوك تماماً
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-restock]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const id = b.dataset.restock;
        if (!id) return;
        window.location.href = 'inventory-movements.html?product_id=' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '&mode=in';
      }));
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-fuel]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const id = b.dataset.fuel;
        if (!id) return;
        window.location.href = 'inventory-movements.html?stock_type=fuel&fuel_type_id=' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '&mode=in';
      }));
      box.querySelectorAll('[data-resolve]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => resolveAlert(b.dataset.resolve)));
    }

    /* ============================================================
       promptModal — بديل مخصص لـ prompt مع نفس السلوك
       ============================================================ */
    function promptModal(defaultValue) {
      return new Promise(resolve => {
        const modal = $('resolveModal');
        const input = $('resolveNote');
        const confirmBtn = $('resolveConfirmBtn');
        if (!modal || !input || !confirmBtn) { resolve(defaultValue); return; }

        input.value = defaultValue || '';
        const cleanup = (value) => {
          modal.classList.remove('show');
          confirmBtn.removeEventListener('click', onConfirm);
          modal.removeEventListener('click', onOverlay);
          input.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
          resolve(value);
        };
        const onConfirm = () => cleanup(input.value);
        const onOverlay = (e) => { if (e.target === modal) cleanup(null); };
        const onKey = (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); onConfirm(); }
          if (e.key === 'Escape') cleanup(null);
        };
        confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', onConfirm);
        modal.addEventListener('click', onOverlay);
        input.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        modal.classList.add('show');
        setTimeout(() => { try { input.focus(); input.setSelectionRange(input.value.length, input.value.length); } catch(e){} }, 350);
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       resolveAlert — نفس المنطق تماماً مع prompt مخصص
       ============================================================ */    async function resolveAlert(id) {
      const note = await promptModal('تمت المراجعة');
      if (note === null) return;
      busy(true);
      try {
        await call('resolveStockAlertRecord', [Number(id), String(note).trim()]);
        toast('تم تسجيل معالجة التنبيه. تحقق من حركة الرصيد الفعلية.');
        await reloadAlerts();
      } catch (e) {
        toast(e.message, true);
      } finally {
        busy(false);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       reloadAlerts — مع تمرير search للـ bridge
       ============================================================ */
    async function reloadAlerts() {
      busy(true);
      try {
        const args = { limit: 0, offset: 0 };
        if (searchQuery) args.search = searchQuery;
        const out = await call('getStockAlertRecords', [JSON.stringify(args)]);
        summary = data(out) || {};
        source = rows(out);
        render();
      } catch (e) {
        toast(e.message || 'تعذر تحميل تنبيهات المخزون', true);
        const box = $('alerts');
        if (box) box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-triangle-exclamation"></i><p>تعذر جلب السجل الحقيقي. راجع اتصال الجسر وقاعدة البيانات.</p></div>';
      } finally {
        busy(false);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Search
       ============================================================ */
    function initSearch() {
      const input = $('searchInput');
      if (!input) return;
      input.addEventListener('input', () => {
        searchQuery = input.value.toLowerCase().trim();
        render(); // فلترة فورية على البيانات المحملة
        clearTimeout(searchTimer);
        searchTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          if (window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.getStockAlertRecords === 'function') {
            reloadAlerts(); // استعلام حقيقي من قاعدة البيانات
          }
        }, 500);
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Sidebar
       ============================================================ */
    function toggleSidebar(open) {
      const sidebar = $('sidebar');
      const toggle = $('sidebarToggle');
      const overlay = $('sidebarOverlay');
      if (!sidebar || !toggle) return;
      const isOpen = typeof open === 'boolean' ? open : !sidebar.classList.contains('open');
      sidebar.classList.toggle('open', isOpen);
      if (overlay) overlay.classList.toggle('show', isOpen);
      toggle.classList.toggle('hidden', isOpen);
    }

    function initSidebar() {
      const toggle = $('sidebarToggle');
      const overlay = $('sidebarOverlay');
      const sidebar = $('sidebar');
      if (toggle) toggle.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar());
      if (overlay) overlay.addEventListener('click', () => toggleSidebar(false));
      if (sidebar) sidebar.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const action = btn.dataset.action;
          toggleSidebar(false);
          setTimeout(() => handleSidebarAction(action), 200);
        });
      });
    }

    function handleSidebarAction(action) {
      switch (action) {
        case 'refresh': reloadAlerts(); break;
        case 'report': openReport(); break;
        case 'restock': goMovements(null, null); break;
        case 'movements': goMovements(null, null); break;
        case 'levels': goLevels(); break;
        case 'export': exportData(); break;
        case 'print': printReport(); break;
        case 'home': goHome(); break;
      }
    }

    function goMovements(productId, fuelId) {
      if (fuelId) {
        window.location.href = 'inventory-movements.html?stock_type=fuel&fuel_type_id=' + encodeURIComponent(fuelId) + '&mode=in';
      } else if (productId) {
        window.location.href = 'inventory-movements.html?product_id=' + encodeURIComponent(productId) + '&mode=in';
      } else {
        window.location.href = 'inventory-movements.html';
      }
    }
    function goLevels() { window.location.href = 'stock-levels.html'; }
    function goHome() {
      // محاولة مسارين لضمان الوصول
      try {
        window.location.href = '../main.html';
      } catch (e) {
        window.location.href = 'main.html';
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Modal Helpers
       ============================================================ */
    function openModal(event, id) {
      if (event && event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
      const el = $(id);
      if (el) el.classList.add('show');
    }
    function closeModal(event, id) {
      if (event && event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
      const el = $(id);
      if (el) el.classList.remove('show');
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Report
       ============================================================ */
    function openReport() {
      const fromEl = $('reportFrom');
      const toEl = $('reportTo');
      if (fromEl && !fromEl.value) fromEl.value = today();
      if (toEl && !toEl.value) toEl.value = today();
      const nameEl = $('reportName');
      if (nameEl && !nameEl.value) nameEl.value = `تقرير التنبيهات ${today()}`;
      const results = $('reportResults');
      if (results) {
        results.innerHTML = `
          <div class="empty-state">
            <i class="fas fa-file-alt"></i>
            <p>اختر الفلاتر ثم اضغط "توليد من المصدر".</p>
          </div>`;
      }
      openModal(null, 'reportModal');
    }

    function generateReport() {
      busy(true);
      try {
        const from = $('reportFrom')?.value || today();
        const to = $('reportTo')?.value || today();
        const type = $('reportType')?.value || 'detailed';
        const levelF = $('reportLevel')?.value || '';
        const resolvedF = $('reportResolved')?.value || '';
        const itemTypeF = $('reportItemType')?.value || '';
        const groupBy = $('reportGroupBy')?.value || 'none';
        const searchQ = ($('reportSearch')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();

        let rowsData = source.slice();

        // فلترة التاريخ
        rowsData = rowsData.filter(r => {
          const d = String(r.created_at || r.resolved_at || '').slice(0, 10);
          if (!d) return true;
          if (from && d < from) return false;
          if (to && d > to) return false;
          return true;
        });
        if (levelF) rowsData = rowsData.filter(r => level(r) === levelF);
        if (resolvedF !== '') rowsData = rowsData.filter(r => Number(r.is_resolved) === Number(resolvedF));
        if (itemTypeF) rowsData = rowsData.filter(r => r.item_type === itemTypeF);
        if (searchQ) {
          rowsData = rowsData.filter(r => {
            const hay = [
              r.product_name || '',
              r.alert_type || '',
              r.alert_level || '',
              r.item_type || '',
              r.resolution_notes || ''
            ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
            return hay.includes(searchQ);
          });
        }

        reportRows = rowsData;

        // إحصاءات
        const total = rowsData.length;
        const criticalCount = rowsData.filter(r => level(r) === 'critical').length;
        const warningCount = rowsData.filter(r => level(r) === 'warning').length;
        const resolvedCount = rowsData.filter(r => Number(r.is_resolved) === 1).length;
        const unresolvedCount = total - resolvedCount;
        const totalShortage = rowsData.reduce((sum, r) => sum + n(r.shortage_quantity), 0);
        const fuelCount = rowsData.filter(r => r.item_type === 'fuel').length;
        const productCount = rowsData.filter(r => r.item_type === 'product').length;

        const summaryHtml = `
          <div class="report-summary">
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">إجمالي التنبيهات</div><div class="value">${total.toLocaleString('en')}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">غير محلولة</div><div class="value warning">${unresolvedCount.toLocaleString('en')}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">حرجة</div><div class="value critical">${criticalCount.toLocaleString('en')}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">تحذيرية</div><div class="value warning">${warningCount.toLocaleString('en')}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">معالجة</div><div class="value good">${resolvedCount.toLocaleString('en')}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">إجمالي العجز</div><div class="value">${fmtNum(totalShortage)}</div></div>
            <div class="report-summary-card"><div class="label">وقود / منتج</div><div class="value">${fuelCount} / ${productCount}</div></div>
          </div>
        `;

        // تجميع
        let groupedHtml = '';
        if (groupBy !== 'none' && rowsData.length) {
          const groups = {};
          rowsData.forEach(r => {
            let key = 'أخرى';
            if (groupBy === 'level') key = level(r) === 'critical' ? 'حرج' : 'تحذير';
            else if (groupBy === 'product') key = r.product_name || 'منتج غير معروف';
            else if (groupBy === 'type') key = alertTypeLabel(r.alert_type);
            else if (groupBy === 'item_type') key = itemTypeLabel(r.item_type);
            if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = { count: 0, shortage: 0, resolved: 0 };
            groups[key].count++;
            groups[key].shortage += n(r.shortage_quantity);
            if (Number(r.is_resolved) === 1) groups[key].resolved++;
          });
          const entries = Object.entries(groups).sort((a, b) => b[1].count - a[1].count);
          groupedHtml = `
            <div class="report-results" style="margin-top:12px;">
              <table>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th style="width:40%;">المجموعة</th>
                    <th style="width:20%;">العدد</th>
                    <th style="width:20%;">العجز</th>
                    <th style="width:20%;">معالج</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  ${entries.map(([k, v]) => `
                    <tr>
                      <td>${esc(k)}</td>
                      <td>${v.count}</td>
                      <td>${fmtNum(v.shortage)}</td>
                      <td>${v.resolved}</td>
                    </tr>
                  `).join('')}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          `;
        }

        // جدول النتائج
        let bodyHtml = '';
        if (type === 'summary') {
          bodyHtml = summaryHtml + groupedHtml;
        } else {
          const tableRows = rowsData.map(r => `
            <tr>
              <td>${esc(r.product_name || '—')}</td>
              <td>${esc(alertTypeLabel(r.alert_type))}</td>
              <td>${level(r) === 'critical' ? 'حرج' : 'تحذير'}</td>
              <td>${fmtNum(r.current_quantity)}</td>
              <td>${fmtNum(r.shortage_quantity)}</td>
              <td>${itemTypeLabel(r.item_type)}</td>
              <td>${Number(r.is_resolved) === 1 ? 'نعم' : 'لا'}</td>
            </tr>
          `).join('');
          const tableHtml = rowsData.length ? `
            <div class="report-results">
              <table>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th style="width:22%;">المنتج</th>
                    <th style="width:15%;">النوع</th>
                    <th style="width:11%;">المستوى</th>
                    <th style="width:13%;">الرصيد</th>
                    <th style="width:13%;">العجز</th>
                    <th style="width:13%;">المصدر</th>
                    <th style="width:13%;">معالج</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>${tableRows}</tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          ` : '<div class="empty-state"><i class="fas fa-search"></i><p>لا توجد نتائج مطابقة للفلاتر.</p></div>';
          bodyHtml = summaryHtml + groupedHtml + tableHtml;
        }

        const results = $('reportResults');
        if (results) results.innerHTML = bodyHtml;
        toast('تم توليد التقرير من المصدر بنجاح');
      } catch (e) {
        toast(e.message || 'فشل توليد التقرير', true);
      } finally {
        busy(false);
      }
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Export CSV — مع نافذة حفظ
       ============================================================ */
    function openSaveFileQuick() {
      if (!reportRows.length && !visible().length) {
        toast('لا توجد بيانات لتصديرها. ولّد التقرير أولاً', true);
        return;
      }
      if (!reportRows.length) reportRows = visible();
      const defaultName = ($('reportName')?.value || `stock_alerts_${today()}`).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, '_');
      const nameInput = $('saveFileName');
      if (nameInput) nameInput.value = defaultName;
      openModal(null, 'saveFileModal');
    }

    function exportData() { openSaveFileQuick(); }

    function initSaveFileModal() {
      const btn = $('confirmSaveBtn');
      if (!btn) return;
      btn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        const rowsData = (reportRows && reportRows.length) ? reportRows : visible();
        if (!rowsData.length) { toast('لا توجد بيانات لتصديرها', true); return; }
        const name = ($('saveFileName')?.value || 'stock_alerts_report').trim() || 'stock_alerts_report';
        const location = $('saveFileLocation')?.value || 'downloads';
        const fileNameCsv = name.endsWith('.csv') ? name : `${name}.csv`;

        // بناء CSV
        const keys = ['id', 'product_name', 'alert_type', 'alert_level', 'item_type', 'current_quantity', 'threshold_quantity', 'shortage_quantity', 'is_resolved', 'resolution_notes', 'created_at', 'resolved_at', 'fuel_type_id', 'product_id'];
        const header = ['المعرف', 'اسم المنتج', 'نوع التنبيه', 'المستوى', 'المصدر', 'الرصيد الحالي', 'حد التنبيه', 'العجز', 'معالج', 'ملاحظات المعالجة', 'تاريخ الإنشاء', 'تاريخ المعالجة', 'معرف الوقود', 'معرف المنتج'];
        const csvRows = [header];
        rowsData.forEach(r => {
          csvRows.push(keys.map(k => {
            let v = r[k];
            if (k === 'is_resolved') return Number(v) === 1 ? 'نعم' : 'لا';
            if (k === 'alert_type') return alertTypeLabel(v);
            if (k === 'alert_level') return v === 'critical' ? 'حرج' : 'تحذير';
            if (k === 'item_type') return itemTypeLabel(v);
            return v == null ? '' : String(v).replace(/"/g, '""');
          }));
        });
        const csv = '\ufeff' + csvRows.map(r => r.map(v => `"${v}"`).join(',')).join('\n');

        if (window.StationExportRuntime && typeof window.StationExportRuntime.openPreparedExport === 'function') {
          window.StationExportRuntime.openPreparedExport({
            blob: new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' }),
            filename: fileNameCsv,
            label: 'تصدير التقرير',
            formats: ['csv', 'xlsx', 'pdf', 'json']
          });
          closeModal(null, 'saveFileModal');
          return;
        }

        busy(true);
        try {
          let saved = false;
          const bridgeMethods = ['saveTextFile', 'saveFileToDownloads', 'saveFile', 'writeTextFileToDownloads'];
          for (const m of bridgeMethods) {
            if (window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface[m] === 'function') {
              try {
                const raw = window.AndroidInterface[m](fileNameCsv, csv, location);
                const result = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
                if (result && result.success !== false) { saved = true; break; }
              } catch (e) { /* try next */ }
            }
          }
          if (!saved) {
            const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = fileNameCsv;
            document.body.appendChild(a);
            a.click();
            document.body.removeChild(a);
            setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
          }
          toast(`تم إنشاء ملف ${fileNameCsv} بنجاح`);
          closeModal(null, 'saveFileModal');
        } catch (e) {
          toast(e.message || 'فشل حفظ الملف', true);
        } finally {
          busy(false);
        }
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Print
       ============================================================ */
    function printReport() {
      const reportName = $('reportName')?.value || `تقرير التنبيهات ${today()}`;
      const ph = $('printHeader');
      if (ph) ph.textContent = reportName;
      const oldTitle = document.title;
      document.title = reportName;
      setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          if (window.AndroidInterface && typeof window.AndroidInterface.printCurrentPage === 'function') {
            window.AndroidInterface.printCurrentPage();
          } else {
            window.print();
          }
        } catch (e) {
          window.print();
        }
        setTimeout(() => { document.title = oldTitle; }, 500);
      }, 100);
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Stat card click handlers
       ============================================================ */
    function initStatCards() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.stat-card').forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const stat = card.dataset.stat;
          if (stat === 'total') { filter = 'all'; }
          else if (stat === 'open') { filter = 'open'; }
          else if (stat === 'critical') { filter = 'critical'; }
          else if (stat === 'resolved') { filter = 'resolved'; }
          const chips = $('filterChips');
          if (chips) {
            chips.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip').forEach(x => {
              x.classList.toggle('active', x.dataset.filter === filter);
            });
          }
          render();
        });
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Top icons + filter chips initialization
       ============================================================ */
    function initUI() {
      const themeBtn = $('themeToggle');
      if (themeBtn) themeBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleTheme);
      const homeBtn = $('homeBtn');
      if (homeBtn) homeBtn.addEventListener('click', goHome);

      // report buttons
      const genBtn = $('generateReportBtn');
      if (genBtn) genBtn.addEventListener('click', generateReport);
      const expBtn = $('exportReportBtn');
      if (expBtn) expBtn.addEventListener('click', exportData);
      const prtBtn = $('printReportBtn');
      if (prtBtn) prtBtn.addEventListener('click', printReport);

      initStatCards();
      initSaveFileModal();
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Escape / Back
       ============================================================ */
    function initKeyboardHandlers() {
      document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
          const anyOpen = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay')).some(x => x.classList.contains('show'));
          const sbOpen = $('sidebar')?.classList.contains('open');
          if (anyOpen) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('show'));
          } else if (sbOpen) {
            toggleSidebar(false);
          }
        }
      });

      history.pushState({ page: 'inventory-alerts' }, '', location.href);
      window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
        const anyOpen = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay')).some(x => x.classList.contains('show'));
        const sbOpen = $('sidebar')?.classList.contains('open');
        if (anyOpen || sbOpen) {
          document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('show'));
          toggleSidebar(false);
          history.pushState({ page: 'inventory-alerts' }, '', location.href);
        }
      });
    }

    /* ============================================================
       Boot
       ============================================================ */
    function boot() {
      try {
        applyTheme();
        renderFilters();
        initSidebar();
        initUI();
        initSearch();
        initKeyboardHandlers();
        reloadAlerts();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('boot error:', err);
        toast('خطأ في التهيئة: ' + (err.message || err), true);
      }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
    } else {
      boot();
    }

    /* Public exports */
    Object.assign(window, {
      reloadAlerts, resolveAlert, generateReport, exportData, printReport,
      openReport, openModal, closeModal, goHome
    });
  })();
  </script>

    <script src="file:///android_asset/assets-local/js/export-runtime.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

package com.aistudio.dieselstationsms.kxmpzq

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.database.Cursor
import android.database.sqlite.SQLiteDatabase
import android.util.Log
import com.aistudio.dieselstationsms.kxmpzq.sms.PumpMeterSmsRepository
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.text.DecimalFormat
import java.text.DecimalFormatSymbols
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.UUID
import kotlin.math.abs

/**
 * Offline-first delivery-fee persistence and accounting.
 *
 * Fuel sale totals remain the fuel-sale amount. Delivery fees are saved separately and
 * recognised through a balanced journal. A driver-borne fee is not posted until an
 * actual driver is assigned to the delivery.
 */
internal fun interface DeliveryLookupQueryRunner {
    fun rawQuery(database: SQLiteDatabase, sql: String, arguments: Array<String>): Cursor
}

internal object DefaultDeliveryLookupQueryRunner : DeliveryLookupQueryRunner {
    override fun rawQuery(database: SQLiteDatabase, sql: String, arguments: Array<String>): Cursor =
        database.rawQuery(sql, arguments)
}

object DeliveryFeeAccounting {
    const val EXEMPT = "exempt"
    const val ADD_TO_SALES = "add_to_sales"
    const val DRIVER = "driver"
    const val DEDUCT_FROM_SALES = "deduct_from_sales"
    private val ensuredSchemaVersions = mutableSetOf<String>()

    private data class Account(
        val id: Long,
        val code: String,
        val name: String,
        val arabicName: String,
        val type: String
    ) {
        val allText: String get() = "$code $name $arabicName".lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        val typeText: String get() = type.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
    }

    data class FuelSale(
        val id: Long,
        val parentSaleId: Long,
        val stationId: Int,
        val customerId: Long,
        val paymentMethod: String,
        val fuelTypeId: Long,
        val fuelTypeName: String,
        val quantity: Double,
        val amount: Double,
        val deliveryFee: Double,
        val deliveryFeeMode: String,
        val deliveryLocation: String,
        val cashCustomerName: String,
        val grossAmount: Double = amount,
        val isFuelSale: Boolean = true
    )

    fun normalizeMode(value: String?): String {
        val v = value.orEmpty().trim().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
        return when (v) {
            "add_to_sales", "added_to_sales", "sales", "add", "تضاف لقيمة المبيعات", "تُضاف لقيمة المبيعات" -> ADD_TO_SALES
            "driver", "on_driver", "driver_account", "على حساب السائق" -> DRIVER
            "deduct_from_sales", "subtract_from_sales", "deduct", "تُنقص من المبيعات", "تنقص من المبيعات" -> DEDUCT_FROM_SALES
            else -> EXEMPT
        }
    }

    fun modeLabel(value: String?): String = when (normalizeMode(value)) {
        ADD_TO_SALES -> "تُضاف لقيمة المبيعات"
        DRIVER -> "على حساب السائق"
        DEDUCT_FROM_SALES -> "تُنقص من المبيعات"
        else -> "معفي منها"
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun ensureSchema(helper: DatabaseHelper) {
        val db = helper.writableDatabase
        val schemaKey = "${db.path}:${db.version}"
        if (schemaKey in ensuredSchemaVersions) {
            // The durable migration marker may be reset independently of this in-memory
            // schema cache. Re-check it before returning so recovery/backfill remains idempotent.
            migrateLegacyDeliveryContext(db)
            return
        }
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "delivery_service_fee", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "delivery_fee_mode", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exempt'")
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "delivery_location", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "cash_customer_name", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "delivery_fee_display_total", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        // Preserve the original fuel quantity/value for auditing and fee reconciliation.
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "gross_amount", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "fuel_sales", "gross_quantity", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "delivery_service_fee", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "delivery_fee_mode", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exempt'")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "delivery_location", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "cash_customer_name", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "delivery_fee_display_total", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "gross_amount", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "sales_transactions", "gross_quantity", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "sale_kind", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'fuel'")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "delivery_service_fee", "REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "delivery_fee_mode", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exempt'")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "delivery_location", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "cash_customer_name", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''")
        addColumn(db, "deliveries", "delivery_fee_status", "TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none'")
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS delivery_service_fee_ledger (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                uuid TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                station_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                fuel_sale_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                parent_sale_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                delivery_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                driver_id INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                amount REAL NOT NULL CHECK(amount >= 0),
                mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exempt',
                status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
                journal_entry_id INTEGER,
                settlement_journal_entry_id INTEGER,
                employee_payment_id INTEGER,
                created_by INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                settled_by INTEGER,
                created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                settled_at TEXT,
                notes TEXT
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )
        db.execSQL(
            "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_delivery_fee_station_sale " +
                "ON delivery_service_fee_ledger(station_id,fuel_sale_id) WHERE fuel_sale_id > 0"
        )
        db.execSQL(
            "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_delivery_fee_station_delivery " +
                "ON delivery_service_fee_ledger(station_id,delivery_id) WHERE delivery_id > 0"
        )
        ensuredSchemaVersions += schemaKey
        migrateLegacyDeliveryContext(db)
    }

    private fun columns(db: SQLiteDatabase, table: String): Set<String> {
        if (!tableExists(db, table)) return emptySet()
        val result = linkedSetOf<String>()
        db.rawQuery("PRAGMA table_info(`$table`)", null).use { cursor ->
            while (cursor.moveToNext()) result += cursor.getString(cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow("name"))
        }
        return result
    }

    private fun tableExists(db: SQLiteDatabase, table: String): Boolean =
        db.rawQuery("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=? LIMIT 1", arrayOf(table))
            .use { it.moveToFirst() }

    private fun addColumn(db: SQLiteDatabase, table: String, name: String, definition: String) {
        if (!tableExists(db, table)) return
        if (name in columns(db, table)) return
        db.execSQL("ALTER TABLE `$table` ADD COLUMN `$name` $definition")
    }

    private fun putIfColumn(db: SQLiteDatabase, table: String, values: ContentValues, name: String, value: Any?) {
        if (name !in columns(db, table)) return
        when (value) {
            null -> values.putNull(name)
            is String -> values.put(name, value)
            is Int -> values.put(name, value)
            is Long -> values.put(name, value)
            is Double -> values.put(name, value)
            is Float -> values.put(name, value)
            is Boolean -> values.put(name, if (value) 1 else 0)
            else -> values.put(name, value.toString())
        }
    }


    private const val LEGACY_DELIVERY_CONTEXT_MIGRATION = "legacy_delivery_context_v1"

    private data class ResolvedDeliveryContext(
        val serviceFee: Double,
        val feeMode: String,
        val location: String,
        val cashCustomerName: String
    )

    private fun firstNonBlank(row: JSONObject?, keys: List<String>): String? {
        if (row == null) return null
        for (key in keys) {
            if (!row.has(key) || row.isNull(key)) continue
            val value = row.optString(key, "").trim()
            if (value.isNotEmpty() && !value.equals("null", ignoreCase = true)) return value
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun firstPositive(row: JSONObject?, keys: List<String>): Double? {
        if (row == null) return null
        for (key in keys) {
            if (!row.has(key) || row.isNull(key)) continue
            val value = row.optDouble(key, Double.NaN)
            if (value.isFinite() && value > 0.0) return value
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun firstNonExemptMode(row: JSONObject?, keys: List<String>): String? {
        if (row == null) return null
        for (key in keys) {
            val raw = firstNonBlank(row, listOf(key)) ?: continue
            val normalized = normalizeMode(raw)
            if (normalized != EXEMPT) return normalized
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun readParentSale(
        db: SQLiteDatabase,
        parentSaleId: Long,
        stationId: Int
    ): JSONObject? {
        if (parentSaleId <= 0L || !tableExists(db, "sales_transactions")) return null
        val parentColumns = columns(db, "sales_transactions")
        if (!setOf("id", "station_id").all { it in parentColumns }) return null
        return db.rawQuery(
            "SELECT * FROM sales_transactions WHERE id=? AND station_id=? LIMIT 1",
            arrayOf(parentSaleId.toString(), stationId.toString())
        ).use { cursor -> if (cursor.moveToFirst()) cursorObject(cursor) else null }
    }

    private fun readLinkedDeliveries(
        db: SQLiteDatabase,
        parentSaleId: Long,
        stationId: Int,
        parentSaleVerified: Boolean
    ): List<JSONObject> {
        if (parentSaleId <= 0L || !tableExists(db, "deliveries")) return emptyList()
        val deliveryColumns = columns(db, "deliveries")
        if (!setOf("id", "sale_id").all { it in deliveryColumns }) return emptyList()
        val parentColumns = columns(db, "sales_transactions")
        val parentCanBeVerified = parentSaleId > 0L &&
            setOf("id", "station_id").all { it in parentColumns }
        if (parentCanBeVerified && !parentSaleVerified) return emptyList()
        if ("station_id" !in deliveryColumns && !parentSaleVerified) return emptyList()

        val predicates = mutableListOf("sale_id=?")
        val arguments = mutableListOf(parentSaleId.toString())
        if ("station_id" in deliveryColumns) {
            if (parentSaleVerified) {
                predicates += "(station_id IS NULL OR station_id=0 OR station_id=?)"
            } else {
                predicates += "station_id=?"
            }
            arguments += stationId.toString()
        }
        if ("is_deleted" in deliveryColumns) predicates += "COALESCE(is_deleted,0)=0"
        if ("deleted_at" in deliveryColumns) predicates += "(deleted_at IS NULL OR TRIM(CAST(deleted_at AS TEXT))='')"
        for (flag in listOf("is_cancelled", "is_canceled", "is_void", "is_voided", "is_invalid", "is_reversed")) {
            if (flag in deliveryColumns) predicates += "COALESCE(" + flag + ",0)=0"
        }
        val orderParts = listOf("delivery_date", "created_at", "id").filter { it in deliveryColumns }
        val order = if (orderParts.isEmpty()) "id DESC" else orderParts.joinToString(", ") { it + " DESC" }
        val sql = "SELECT * FROM deliveries WHERE " + predicates.joinToString(" AND ") + " ORDER BY " + order
        return db.rawQuery(sql, arguments.toTypedArray()).use { cursor ->
            buildList {
                while (cursor.moveToNext()) add(cursorObject(cursor))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun readDeliveryFeeLedger(
        db: SQLiteDatabase,
        fuelSaleId: Long,
        parentSaleId: Long,
        stationId: Int
    ): JSONObject? {
        if (!tableExists(db, "delivery_service_fee_ledger")) return null
        val ledgerColumns = columns(db, "delivery_service_fee_ledger")
        if (!setOf("station_id", "amount", "mode").all { it in ledgerColumns }) return null
        val salePredicates = mutableListOf<String>()
        val arguments = mutableListOf(stationId.toString())
        if ("fuel_sale_id" in ledgerColumns && fuelSaleId > 0L) {
            salePredicates += "(fuel_sale_id=? AND fuel_sale_id>0)"
            arguments += fuelSaleId.toString()
        }
        if ("parent_sale_id" in ledgerColumns && parentSaleId > 0L) {
            salePredicates += "(parent_sale_id=? AND parent_sale_id>0)"
            arguments += parentSaleId.toString()
        }
        if (salePredicates.isEmpty()) return null
        val active = if ("status" in ledgerColumns) " AND LOWER(COALESCE(status,'')) NOT IN ('reversed','cancelled','void')" else ""
        val order = if ("id" in ledgerColumns) "id DESC" else "rowid DESC"
        val sql = "SELECT * FROM delivery_service_fee_ledger WHERE station_id=? AND (" +
            salePredicates.joinToString(" OR ") + ")" + active + " ORDER BY " + order + " LIMIT 1"
        return db.rawQuery(sql, arguments.toTypedArray()).use { cursor ->
            if (cursor.moveToFirst()) cursorObject(cursor) else null
        }
    }

    /**
     * The single source-resolution contract used by migration and every fuel-sale
     * lookup. Fallbacks are constrained to the same fuel row, invoice and station.
     */
    private fun resolveDeliveryContext(
        db: SQLiteDatabase,
        fuelSale: JSONObject,
        stationId: Int
    ): ResolvedDeliveryContext {
        val fuelSaleId = fuelSale.optLong("id", 0L)
        val parentSaleId = fuelSale.optLong("sale_id", 0L)
        val parent = readParentSale(db, parentSaleId, stationId)
        val deliveries = readLinkedDeliveries(db, parentSaleId, stationId, parent != null)
        val ledger = readDeliveryFeeLedger(db, fuelSaleId, parentSaleId, stationId)

        val fee = firstPositive(fuelSale, listOf("delivery_service_fee"))
            ?: firstPositive(parent, listOf("delivery_service_fee"))
            ?: ledger?.let { it.optDouble("amount", 0.0).takeIf { value -> value.isFinite() && value >= 0.0 } }
            ?: firstPositive(fuelSale, listOf("service_fee", "delivery_fee_amount"))
            ?: firstPositive(parent, listOf("service_fee", "delivery_fee_amount"))
            ?: deliveries.firstNotNullOfOrNull {
                firstPositive(it, listOf("delivery_service_fee", "service_fee", "delivery_fee_amount"))
            }
            ?: 0.0

        val childMode = firstNonExemptMode(fuelSale, listOf("delivery_fee_mode"))
        val parentMode = firstNonExemptMode(parent, listOf("delivery_fee_mode"))
        val ledgerMode = ledger?.let { firstNonBlank(it, listOf("mode"))?.let(::normalizeMode) }
        val legacyMode = if (ledger == null) {
            firstNonExemptMode(fuelSale, listOf("fee_mode", "delivery_fee_calculation"))
                ?: firstNonExemptMode(parent, listOf("fee_mode", "delivery_fee_calculation"))
                ?: deliveries.firstNotNullOfOrNull {
                    firstNonExemptMode(it, listOf("fee_mode", "delivery_fee_calculation"))
                }
        } else {
            null
        }
        val mode = childMode ?: parentMode ?: ledgerMode ?: legacyMode ?: EXEMPT

        val locationKeys = listOf("delivery_location", "location", "delivery_address")
        val location = firstNonBlank(fuelSale, locationKeys)
            ?: firstNonBlank(parent, locationKeys)
            ?: deliveries.firstNotNullOfOrNull { firstNonBlank(it, locationKeys) }
            ?: ""

        val cashNameKeys = listOf("cash_customer_name", "cash_customer_display_name", "customer_cash_name")
        val cashName = firstNonBlank(fuelSale, cashNameKeys)
            ?: firstNonBlank(parent, cashNameKeys)
            ?: deliveries.firstNotNullOfOrNull { firstNonBlank(it, cashNameKeys) }
            ?: ""

        return ResolvedDeliveryContext(fee, mode, location, cashName)
    }

    private fun updateContextDefaults(
        db: SQLiteDatabase,
        table: String,
        row: JSONObject,
        values: ContentValues,
        context: ResolvedDeliveryContext
    ) {
        val tableColumns = columns(db, table)
        if ("delivery_service_fee" in tableColumns &&
            row.optDouble("delivery_service_fee", 0.0) <= 0.0 && context.serviceFee > 0.0
        ) {
            values.put("delivery_service_fee", context.serviceFee)
        }
        if ("delivery_fee_mode" in tableColumns &&
            normalizeMode(row.optString("delivery_fee_mode", EXEMPT)) == EXEMPT &&
            context.feeMode != EXEMPT && context.serviceFee > 0.0
        ) {
            values.put("delivery_fee_mode", context.feeMode)
        }
        if ("delivery_location" in tableColumns &&
            row.optString("delivery_location", "").isBlank() && context.location.isNotBlank()
        ) {
            values.put("delivery_location", context.location)
        }
        if ("cash_customer_name" in tableColumns &&
            row.optString("cash_customer_name", "").isBlank() && context.cashCustomerName.isNotBlank()
        ) {
            values.put("cash_customer_name", context.cashCustomerName)
        }
    }

    /**
     * One-time, restart-safe historical backfill. It only fills canonical defaults;
     * it never changes non-zero fees, non-exempt modes or non-empty text.
     * It creates no journal, payment, balance, stock or ledger records.
     */
    private fun migrateLegacyDeliveryContext(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
        if (!tableExists(db, "fuel_sales")) return
        db.execSQL(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS delivery_context_migrations (
                migration_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
                completed_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                migrated_rows INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
            )
            """.trimIndent()
        )
        val alreadyDone = db.rawQuery(
            "SELECT 1 FROM delivery_context_migrations WHERE migration_key=? LIMIT 1",
            arrayOf(LEGACY_DELIVERY_CONTEXT_MIGRATION)
        ).use { it.moveToFirst() }
        if (alreadyDone) return

        val ownsTransaction = !db.inTransaction()
        if (ownsTransaction) db.beginTransaction()
        try {
            val fuelRows = db.rawQuery("SELECT * FROM fuel_sales ORDER BY id", null).use { cursor ->
                buildList {
                    while (cursor.moveToNext()) add(cursorObject(cursor))
                }
            }
            val parentColumns = columns(db, "sales_transactions")
            val deliveryColumns = columns(db, "deliveries")
            var migratedRows = 0

            for (fuelRow in fuelRows) {
                val fuelSaleId = fuelRow.optLong("id", 0L)
                val parentSaleId = fuelRow.optLong("sale_id", 0L)
                val stationId = fuelRow.optInt("station_id", 0)
                if (fuelSaleId <= 0L || stationId <= 0) continue

                val context = resolveDeliveryContext(db, fuelRow, stationId)
                val fuelValues = ContentValues()
                updateContextDefaults(db, "fuel_sales", fuelRow, fuelValues, context)
                if (fuelValues.size() > 0) {
                    db.update(
                        "fuel_sales", fuelValues, "id=? AND station_id=?",
                        arrayOf(fuelSaleId.toString(), stationId.toString())
                    )
                    migratedRows++
                }

                val parent = readParentSale(db, parentSaleId, stationId)
                if (parent != null && parentColumns.isNotEmpty()) {
                    val parentValues = ContentValues()
                    updateContextDefaults(db, "sales_transactions", parent, parentValues, context)
                    if (parentValues.size() > 0) {
                        db.update(
                            "sales_transactions", parentValues, "id=? AND station_id=?",
                            arrayOf(parentSaleId.toString(), stationId.toString())
                        )
                        migratedRows++
                    }
                }

                val linkedDeliveries = readLinkedDeliveries(db, parentSaleId, stationId, parent != null)
                for (delivery in linkedDeliveries) {
                    val deliveryId = delivery.optLong("id", 0L)
                    if (deliveryId <= 0L || deliveryColumns.isEmpty()) continue
                    val deliveryValues = ContentValues()
                    val ownLegacyLocation = firstNonBlank(delivery, listOf("location", "delivery_address"))
                    val ownLegacyCashName = firstNonBlank(delivery, listOf("cash_customer_display_name", "customer_cash_name"))
                    if ("delivery_location" in deliveryColumns &&
                        delivery.optString("delivery_location", "").isBlank()
                    ) {
                        (ownLegacyLocation ?: context.location.takeIf { linkedDeliveries.size == 1 })
                            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                            ?.let { deliveryValues.put("delivery_location", it) }
                    }
                    if ("cash_customer_name" in deliveryColumns &&
                        delivery.optString("cash_customer_name", "").isBlank()
                    ) {
                        (ownLegacyCashName ?: context.cashCustomerName)
                            .takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                            ?.let { deliveryValues.put("cash_customer_name", it) }
                    }

                    val deliveryLedger = db.rawQuery(
                        "SELECT amount,mode FROM delivery_service_fee_ledger WHERE station_id=? AND delivery_id=? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
                        arrayOf(stationId.toString(), deliveryId.toString())
                    ).use { cursor ->
                        if (cursor.moveToFirst()) JSONObject().apply {
                            put("amount", cursor.getDouble(0))
                            put("mode", cursor.getString(1).orEmpty())
                        } else null
                    }
                    if ("delivery_service_fee" in deliveryColumns &&
                        delivery.optDouble("delivery_service_fee", 0.0) <= 0.0
                    ) {
                        val ledgerFee = firstPositive(deliveryLedger, listOf("amount"))
                        val ownLegacyFee = if (deliveryLedger == null) {
                            firstPositive(delivery, listOf("service_fee", "delivery_fee_amount"))
                        } else null
                        val resolvedFee = ledgerFee ?: ownLegacyFee
                            ?: context.serviceFee.takeIf { deliveryLedger == null && linkedDeliveries.size == 1 }
                        resolvedFee?.let { deliveryValues.put("delivery_service_fee", it) }
                    }
                    if ("delivery_fee_mode" in deliveryColumns &&
                        normalizeMode(delivery.optString("delivery_fee_mode", EXEMPT)) == EXEMPT
                    ) {
                        val ledgerModeValue = deliveryLedger?.let { firstNonBlank(it, listOf("mode")) }
                        val ownLegacyMode = if (deliveryLedger == null) {
                            firstNonExemptMode(delivery, listOf("fee_mode", "delivery_fee_calculation"))
                        } else null
                        val resolvedMode = ledgerModeValue ?: ownLegacyMode
                            ?: context.feeMode.takeIf {
                                deliveryLedger == null && context.serviceFee > 0.0 && linkedDeliveries.size == 1
                            }
                        resolvedMode?.let { deliveryValues.put("delivery_fee_mode", it) }
                    }

                    if (deliveryValues.size() > 0) {
                        val where = if ("station_id" in deliveryColumns) {
                            if (parent != null) "id=? AND (station_id=? OR station_id IS NULL OR station_id=0)" else "id=? AND station_id=?"
                        } else {
                            "id=?"
                        }
                        val arguments = if ("station_id" in deliveryColumns) {
                            arrayOf(deliveryId.toString(), stationId.toString())
